Perfect! I now have a comprehensive understanding of the codebase. Let me compile my findings:

## Survey Report: Collections Feature for Agentcenter Nuxt

I've completed a thorough exploration of the codebase. Here are my findings organized by your request:

---

### 1. **Database Schema** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/db/schema/`

**Good news:** A `collection.ts` schema already exists! The foundation is already laid.

**Collections tables:**
```typescript
// /shared/db/schema/collection.ts
export const collections = pgTable("collections", {
  id: text().primaryKey(),                    // UUID
  ownerUserId: text().notNull(),              // FK → users.id
  name: text().notNull(),                     // English name
  nameZh: text(),                             // Bilingual support (Chinese)
  systemKind: systemKindEnum(),               // "installed" | "saved" (system auto-managed)
  createdAt: timestamp(),                     // Default: now()
});

export const collectionItems = pgTable("collection_items", {
  collectionId: text().notNull(),             // FK → collections.id
  extensionId: text().notNull(),              // FK → extensions.id
  addedAt: timestamp(),                       // Default: now()
  // Composite PK: (collectionId, extensionId)
});
```

**Related tables referenced:**
- `extensions` — `/shared/db/schema/extension.ts` — uses UUID text IDs, has i18n columns (`name`/`nameZh`, `description`/`descriptionZh`, `tagline`/`taglineZh`), visibility enum (`draft|published|archived`), scope enum (`personal|org|enterprise`)
- `extensionTags` — many-to-many join table with `tags` table; model for collections relationships
- `users` — `/shared/db/schema/auth.ts` — `id` (text), `email`, `name`, `locale`, `themePreference`, `defaultDeptId`
- `installs` — `/shared/db/schema/activity.ts` — tracks install records, `uninstalledAt` for soft-deletes
- `tags` — `id` (text key), `labelEn`, `labelZh`

**Schema patterns:**
- **IDs:** Text-based UUIDs (not numeric CUID/serial)
- **Timestamps:** Always `timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow()` with ISO string serialization
- **Soft deletes:** Use nullable `uninstalledAt` column (as in `installs` table), not boolean flags
- **Bilingual:** Parallel `{field}` and `{field}Zh` columns (e.g., `name`/`nameZh`)
- **Multi-tenancy:** `ownerUserId` (user-scoped) or `ownerOrgId` (org-scoped) patterns; no soft-delete column seen
- **System-managed:** `systemKind` enum distinguishes auto-managed (Installed/Saved) from user-created collections

---

### 2. **Public API** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/v1/`

All routes are **read-only** for external/CLI clients. Response shape is consistent:

**GET `/api/v1/extensions`** — List extensions with filtering
```typescript
Response: {
  items: [{ slug, name, nameZh, category, scope, badge, description, descriptionZh, tags, funcCat, subCat, l2, downloadsCount, starsAvg }],
  total: number,
  page: number,
  pageSize: number
}
Cache-Control: "public, s-maxage=60, stale-while-revalidate=300"
```

**GET `/api/v1/extensions/[slug]`** — Single extension detail
```typescript
Response: {
  slug, name, nameZh, category, scope, badge, tagline, description, descriptionZh, tags,
  funcCat, subCat, l2, license, homepageUrl, repoUrl, compatibilityJson,
  downloadsCount, starsAvg (string "0.0"), ratingsCount, publishedAt, version, bundleUrl
}
```

**POST `/api/v1/installs`** — Record an install (Bearer token auth)
```typescript
Body: { extensionSlug: string, version?: string, agentName?: string, agentVersion?: string }
Response: { ok: true, installId: string, isFirstInstall: boolean, version: string }
Auth: Bearer token (from sessions table)
Error format: { statusCode: number, statusMessage: code, data: { error: code, message } }
```

**Patterns:**
- All return `{ items, total, page, pageSize }` for lists
- Errors: `{ statusCode, statusMessage, data: { error, message } }`
- Auth: Bearer token via `authenticateBearerToken(event)` helper
- Cache headers on read routes (public, s-maxage=60)

---

### 3. **Internal API** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/`

These are form-backing endpoints for authenticated web users. Two representative patterns:

**POST `/api/internal/installs`** — Save/install an extension
```typescript
// /server/api/internal/installs.post.ts
const user = await requireUser(event)  // Throws 401 if not authenticated
const body = await readValidatedBody(event, (raw) => InstallBody.parse(raw))
// Body: { extensionId: string, version?: string }
// Returns: { ok: true, ...result }
// Errors: InstallError codes → statusCode (404 not_found, 422 uninstallable)
```

**POST `/api/internal/publish/submit.post`** — Submit extension for review
```typescript
// /server/api/internal/publish/submit.post.ts
const user = await requireUser(event)
const body = await readValidatedBody(event, (raw) => bodySchema.parse(raw))
// Body: { versionId: string (UUID) }
// Returns: { ok: true, ... }
// Errors: Custom error codes → statusCode mapping
```

**Patterns:**
- All use `requireUser(event)` → throws 401 if no session
- Body validation: Zod with `readValidatedBody()`
- Custom error codes map to HTTP status codes
- Return shape: `{ ok: true, ...result }` or throw `createError()`

---

### 4. **Auth** — How to get current user

**In server routes:**
```typescript
// /server/utils/auth.ts
export async function requireUser(event: H3Event): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const user = await getSessionUser(event)
  if (!user) throw createError({ statusCode: 401, statusMessage: "Unauthenticated" })
  return user
}

export async function getSessionUser(event: H3Event): Promise<SessionUser | null> {
  const auth = useAuthServer()
  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: toFetchHeaders(...) })
  return session?.user ?? null
}

// SessionUser type is from Better Auth
type SessionUser = { id, email, name, ... }
```

**For Bearer tokens (CLI/v1 API):**
```typescript
// /server/utils/api-auth.ts
export async function authenticateBearerToken(event: H3Event): Promise<ApiUser | null> {
  // Checks Authorization: Bearer <token> header
  // Looks up in sessions table, validates expiry
  // Returns { id, email, name, defaultDeptId } or null
}
```

**Framework:** Better Auth (`betterAuth`) with `drizzleAdapter`. User schema extended with `locale`, `themePreference`, `defaultDeptId`.

---

### 5. **Existing Save/Favorite/Collection Feature** — Already Live!

A "Saved" system is **already implemented**:

**SaveButton component:**
- `/app/components/extension/SaveButton.vue` — Bookmark-style button on extension detail page
- Posts to `/api/internal/installs` (reuses the install endpoint for saving)
- Shows transient "Saved" state (3s timeout) after click

**Profile "Saved" section:**
- Visible in profile at `/profile?section=saved`
- Backed by `getSavedForUser()` query in `/server/utils/queries/profile.ts`
- Displays extensions saved to the user's `systemKind: "saved"` collection
- Query joins `collectionItems → collections (systemKind='saved')` to get list

**How it works:**
1. Click SaveButton on `/extensions/[slug]` → POST `/api/internal/installs` with `{ extensionId }`
2. Backend uses `recordInstall()` orchestrator which:
   - Gets or creates the user's "Saved" system collection
   - Records the install/save in `installs` and `collectionItems` tables
3. Profile page shows all items in that collection via `SectionSaved.vue`

**Tables used:**
- `collections` (one row per user with `systemKind: 'saved'`)
- `collectionItems` (one row per saved extension)
- `installs` (tracks the event; same table used for both installs and saves)

### 8. **Shared Validators** — Zod Schema Style

**File:** `/shared/validators/`

Examples show the pattern:

```typescript
// /shared/validators/profile.ts
export const PROFILE_NAME_MAX = 80
export const ProfileFormSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1).max(80),
  defaultDeptId: z.string().max(120).default(""),
  bio: z.string().max(280).default(""),
})
export type ProfileFormValues = z.infer<typeof ProfileFormSchema>

// /shared/validators/manifest.ts
export const SLUG_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/
export const ExtensionManifestCore = z.object({
  slug: z.string().min(3).max(64).regex(SLUG_PATTERN, "..."),
  name: z.string().min(2).max(80),
  nameZh: z.string().max(80).optional(),
  ...
})
```

**For collections, you'd add:**
```typescript
// /shared/validators/collections.ts
export const COLLECTION_NAME_MIN = 1
export const COLLECTION_NAME_MAX = 100

export const CreateCollectionSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(COLLECTION_NAME_MIN).max(COLLECTION_NAME_MAX),
  nameZh: z.string().max(COLLECTION_NAME_MAX).optional(),
  isPublic: z.boolean().default(false),
})

export const UpdateCollectionSchema = CreateCollectionSchema.partial()

export type CreateCollectionInput = z.infer<typeof CreateCollectionSchema>
```

---

### 9. **Tags Table** — Many-to-Many Pattern

**Schema:** `/shared/db/schema/extension.ts`

```typescript
export const tags = pgTable("tags", {
  id: text().primaryKey(),              // Tag key: "real-time", "async", ...
  labelEn: text().notNull(),
  labelZh: text().notNull(),
});

export const extensionTags = pgTable("extension_tags", {
  extensionId: text().notNull().references(() => extensions.id),
  tagId: text().notNull().references(() => tags.id),
  // Composite PK: (extensionId, tagId)
}, (t) => [
  primaryKey({ columns: [t.extensionId, t.tagId] }),
  index("idx_extension_tags_tag_ext").on(t.tagId, t.extensionId),
]);
```

**Collections would follow the same pattern** (already does with `collectionItems`):
```typescript
// Already in collection.ts:
export const collectionItems = pgTable("collection_items", {
  collectionId: text().notNull().references(() => collections.id),
  extensionId: text().notNull().references(() => extensions.id),
  addedAt: timestamp(),
}, (t) => [primaryKey({ columns: [t.collectionId, t.extensionId] })]);
```

1	import { z } from "zod"

3	const InstallBody = z.object({
4	  extensionId: z.string().min(1),
5	  version: z.string().optional(),
6	})
7	
8	export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
9	  const user = await requireUser(event)
10	  const body = await readValidatedBody(event, (raw) => InstallBody.parse(raw))
11	
12	  try {
13	    const result = await recordInstall({
14	      userId: user.id,
15	      extension: { id: body.extensionId },
16	      source: "web",
17	      version: body.version,
18	    })
19	    return { ok: true, ...result }
20	  } catch (err) {
21	    if (err instanceof InstallError) {
22	      const status = err.code === "extension_not_found" ? 404 : 422
23	      throw createError({ statusCode: status, statusMessage: err.code })
24	    }
25	    console.error("internal recordInstall failed", err)
26	    throw createError({ statusCode: 500, statusMessage: "install_failed" })
27	  }
28	})

/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/docs/plan.md:187:**API routes vs server actions**: Nuxt has no server actions. The Next.js implementation used server actions for user-initiated mutations from RSC pages (install toggle from web, save-to-collection, create collection, draft create/update). All of these become POST handlers under `server/api/internal/...` called via `$fetch` from client components. Validation lives in the handler using the same Zod schemas as before; the handler is the new trust boundary. API routes under `/api/v1/...` (public, CLI-consumed), `/api/auth/...` (Better Auth), `/api/upload/sign` (presigned URL), and `/api/inngest` (webhook) keep their original purpose.

1	import { and, desc, eq, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
2	
3	import { useDb } from "~~/server/utils/db"
4	import { installs, ratings } from "~~/shared/db/schema/activity"
5	import { collectionItems, collections } from "~~/shared/db/schema/collection"
6	import { extensionVersions, extensions } from "~~/shared/db/schema/extension"
7	
8	export interface ProfileInstalledRow {
9	  extensionId: string
10	  slug: string
11	  name: string
12	  category: string
13	  iconColor: string | null
14	  installedVersion: string
15	  installedAt: Date
16	}
17	
18	export interface ProfileSavedRow {
19	  extensionId: string
20	  slug: string
21	  name: string
22	  category: string
23	  iconColor: string | null
24	  savedAt: Date
25	}
26	
27	export interface ProfileDraftRow {
28	  extensionId: string
29	  slug: string
30	  name: string
31	  category: string
32	  iconColor: string | null
33	  updatedAt: Date
34	  latestStatus: string | null
35	}
36	
37	export interface ProfilePublishedRow {
38	  extensionId: string
39	  slug: string
40	  name: string
41	  category: string
42	  iconColor: string | null
43	  latestVersion: string | null
44	  downloadsCount: number
45	  starsAvg: string
46	  ratingsCount: number
47	}
48	
49	export type ProfileActivityEvent =
50	  | { kind: "installed"; at: Date; extensionId: string; slug: string; name: string; version: string }
51	  | { kind: "published"; at: Date; extensionId: string; slug: string; name: string; version: string }
52	  | { kind: "rated"; at: Date; extensionId: string; slug: string; name: string; stars: number }
53	
54	export interface ProfileStats {
55	  installedCount: number
56	  publishedCount: number
57	  totalInstallsOfMine: number
58	  avgRatingOfMine: number | null
59	}
60	
61	const ACTIVITY_LIMIT = 20
62	
63	export async function getInstalledForUser(userId: string): Promise<ProfileInstalledRow[]> {
64	  // Active installs only — uninstalledAt IS NULL.
65	  const db = useDb()
66	  return db
67	    .select({
68	      extensionId: extensions.id,
69	      slug: extensions.slug,
70	      name: extensions.name,
71	      category: extensions.category,
72	      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,
73	      installedVersion: installs.version,
74	      installedAt: installs.installedAt,
75	    })
76	    .from(installs)
77	    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, installs.extensionId))
78	    .where(and(eq(installs.userId, userId), isNull(installs.uninstalledAt)))
79	    .orderBy(desc(installs.installedAt))
80	}
81	
82	export async function getSavedForUser(userId: string): Promise<ProfileSavedRow[]> {
83	  const db = useDb()
84	  return db
85	    .select({
86	      extensionId: extensions.id,
87	      slug: extensions.slug,
88	      name: extensions.name,
89	      category: extensions.category,
90	      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,
91	      savedAt: collectionItems.addedAt,
92	    })
93	    .from(collectionItems)
94	    .innerJoin(
95	      collections,
96	      and(
97	        eq(collections.id, collectionItems.collectionId),
98	        eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId),
99	        eq(collections.systemKind, "saved"),
100	      ),
101	    )
102	    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, collectionItems.extensionId))
103	    .orderBy(desc(collectionItems.addedAt))
104	}
105	
106	export async function getDraftsForUser(userId: string): Promise<ProfileDraftRow[]> {
107	  const db = useDb()
108	  const rows = await db
109	    .select({
110	      extensionId: extensions.id,
111	      slug: extensions.slug,
112	      name: extensions.name,
113	      category: extensions.category,
114	      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,
115	      updatedAt: extensions.updatedAt,
116	      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,
117	    })
118	    .from(extensions)
119	    .leftJoin(extensionVersions, eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id))
120	    .where(and(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId), eq(extensions.visibility, "draft")))
121	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.updatedAt), desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))
122	
123	  const seen = new Set<string>()
124	  const out: ProfileDraftRow[] = []
125	  for (const r of rows) {
126	    if (seen.has(r.extensionId)) continue
127	    seen.add(r.extensionId)
128	    out.push(r)
129	  }
130	  return out
131	}
132	
133	export async function getPublishedForUser(userId: string): Promise<ProfilePublishedRow[]> {
134	  // Latest published version per extension. Drizzle doesn't expose DISTINCT
135	  // ON cleanly, so we ORDER BY publishedAt desc and collapse duplicates
136	  // client-side.
137	  const db = useDb()
138	  const rows = await db
139	    .select({
140	      extensionId: extensions.id,
141	      slug: extensions.slug,
142	      name: extensions.name,
143	      category: extensions.category,
144	      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,
145	      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,
146	      downloadsCount: extensions.downloadsCount,
147	      starsAvg: extensions.starsAvg,
148	      ratingsCount: extensions.ratingsCount,
149	    })
150	    .from(extensions)
151	    .leftJoin(
152	      extensionVersions,
153	      and(eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id), eq(extensionVersions.status, "ready")),
154	    )
155	    .where(and(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId), eq(extensions.visibility, "published")))
156	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.publishedAt), desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt))
157	
158	  const seen = new Set<string>()
159	  const out: ProfilePublishedRow[] = []
160	  for (const r of rows) {
161	    if (seen.has(r.extensionId)) continue
162	    seen.add(r.extensionId)
163	    out.push(r)
164	  }
165	  return out
166	}
167	
168	export async function getActivityForUser(userId: string): Promise<ProfileActivityEvent[]> {
169	  // Activity is a union of three event sources (installs, my published
170	  // versions, my ratings). No dedicated activity_log table yet — merge
171	  // in memory rather than a heavyweight UNION ALL.
172	  const db = useDb()
173	  const [installRows, pubRows, ratingRows] = await Promise.all([
174	    db
175	      .select({
176	        at: installs.installedAt,
177	        extensionId: extensions.id,
178	        slug: extensions.slug,
179	        name: extensions.name,
180	        version: installs.version,
181	      })
182	      .from(installs)
183	      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, installs.extensionId))
184	      .where(eq(installs.userId, userId))
185	      .orderBy(desc(installs.installedAt))
186	      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),
187	    db
188	      .select({
189	        at: extensionVersions.publishedAt,
190	        extensionId: extensions.id,
191	        slug: extensions.slug,
192	        name: extensions.name,
193	        version: extensionVersions.version,
194	      })
195	      .from(extensionVersions)
196	      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, extensionVersions.extensionId))
197	      // `status = ready` alone isn't enough: a ready version on a still-
198	      // draft extension hasn't been published publicly yet.
199	      .where(
200	        and(
201	          eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId),
202	          eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
203	          eq(extensionVersions.status, "ready"),
204	        ),
205	      )
206	      .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.publishedAt))
207	      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),
208	    db
209	      .select({
210	        at: ratings.createdAt,
211	        extensionId: extensions.id,
212	        slug: extensions.slug,
213	        name: extensions.name,
214	        stars: ratings.stars,
215	      })
216	      .from(ratings)
217	      .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, ratings.extensionId))
218	      .where(eq(ratings.userId, userId))
219	      .orderBy(desc(ratings.createdAt))
220	      .limit(ACTIVITY_LIMIT),
221	  ])
222	
223	  const merged: ProfileActivityEvent[] = [
224	    ...installRows
225	      .filter((r): r is typeof r & { at: Date } => r.at != null)
226	      .map((r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({
227	        kind: "installed",
228	        at: r.at,
229	        extensionId: r.extensionId,
230	        slug: r.slug,
231	        name: r.name,
232	        version: r.version,
233	      })),
234	    ...pubRows
235	      .filter((r): r is typeof r & { at: Date } => r.at != null)
236	      .map((r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({
237	        kind: "published",
238	        at: r.at,
239	        extensionId: r.extensionId,
240	        slug: r.slug,
241	        name: r.name,
242	        version: r.version,
243	      })),
244	    ...ratingRows
245	      .filter((r): r is typeof r & { at: Date } => r.at != null)
246	      .map((r): ProfileActivityEvent => ({
247	        kind: "rated",
248	        at: r.at,
249	        extensionId: r.extensionId,
250	        slug: r.slug,
251	        name: r.name,
252	        stars: r.stars,
253	      })),
254	  ]
255	
256	  merged.sort((a, b) => b.at.getTime() - a.at.getTime())
257	  return merged.slice(0, ACTIVITY_LIMIT)
258	}
259	
260	export async function getProfileStats(userId: string): Promise<ProfileStats> {
261	  // Two small aggregates, run in parallel.
262	  const db = useDb()
263	  const [installedAgg, pubAgg] = await Promise.all([
264	    db
265	      .select({ c: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
266	      .from(installs)
267	      .where(and(eq(installs.userId, userId), isNull(installs.uninstalledAt))),
268	    db
269	      .select({
270	        count: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
271	        totalInstalls: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${extensions.downloadsCount}),0)::int`,
272	        // Weighted average — an extension with 100 ratings counts 100× more
273	        // than one with a single rating. Null when no rated extensions exist.
274	        weightedStars: sql<string | null>`case when sum(${extensions.ratingsCount}) = 0 then null else sum(${extensions.starsAvg}::numeric * ${extensions.ratingsCount})::numeric / sum(${extensions.ratingsCount}) end`,
275	      })
276	      .from(extensions)
277	      .where(and(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId), eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))),
278	  ])
279	
280	  const pub = pubAgg[0] ?? { count: 0, totalInstalls: 0, weightedStars: null }
281	  return {
282	    installedCount: installedAgg[0]?.c ?? 0,
283	    publishedCount: pub.count,
284	    totalInstallsOfMine: pub.totalInstalls,
285	    avgRatingOfMine: pub.weightedStars != null ? Number(pub.weightedStars) : null,
286	  }
287	}
288

1	import {
2	  getActivityForUser,
3	  getDraftsForUser,
4	  getInstalledForUser,
5	  getPublishedForUser,
6	  getSavedForUser,
7	} from "~~/server/utils/queries/profile"
8	import type {
9	  ProfileActivityEvent,
10	  ProfileDraftRow,
11	  ProfileInstalledRow,
12	  ProfilePublishedRow,
13	  ProfileSavedRow,
14	} from "~~/shared/types"
15	
16	export type SectionKey = "installed" | "published" | "drafts" | "saved" | "activity"
17	
18	const KEYS: ReadonlySet<SectionKey> = new Set(["installed", "published", "drafts", "saved", "activity"])
19	
20	export type SectionResponse =
21	  | { section: "installed"; rows: ProfileInstalledRow[] }
22	  | { section: "published"; rows: ProfilePublishedRow[] }
23	  | { section: "drafts"; rows: ProfileDraftRow[] }
24	  | { section: "saved"; rows: ProfileSavedRow[] }
25	  | { section: "activity"; rows: ProfileActivityEvent[] }
26	
27	// Map Date fields to ISO strings so the endpoint's return type matches what
28	// crosses the wire. JSON serialization would convert these anyway, but typing
29	// them as Date and serializing them as string is a footgun that hides
30	// type/runtime drift.
31	export default defineEventHandler(async (event): Promise<SectionResponse> => {
32	  const session = await requireUser(event)
33	  const raw = getQuery(event).section
34	  const section = typeof raw === "string" ? raw : "installed"
35	  if (!KEYS.has(section as SectionKey)) {
36	    throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: "unknown_section" })
37	  }
38	
39	  switch (section as SectionKey) {
40	    case "installed": {
41	      const rows = await getInstalledForUser(session.id)
42	      return {
43	        section: "installed",
44	        rows: rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, installedAt: r.installedAt.toISOString() })),
45	      }
46	    }
47	    case "published":
48	      // No Date fields on this row.
49	      return { section: "published", rows: await getPublishedForUser(session.id) }
50	    case "drafts": {
51	      const rows = await getDraftsForUser(session.id)
52	      return {
53	        section: "drafts",
54	        rows: rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, updatedAt: r.updatedAt.toISOString() })),
55	      }
56	    }
57	    case "saved": {
58	      const rows = await getSavedForUser(session.id)
59	      return {
60	        section: "saved",
61	        rows: rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, savedAt: r.savedAt.toISOString() })),
62	      }
63	    }
64	    case "activity": {
65	      const rows = await getActivityForUser(session.id)
66	      return {
67	        section: "activity",
68	        rows: rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, at: r.at.toISOString() }) as ProfileActivityEvent),
69	      }
70	    }
71	  }
72	})
73

          "description": "Two-state visibility (private/public). Public collections are discoverable via a /collections page (browse all public collections) and accessible at /collections/[id-or-slug]. Simple, matches typical marketplace expectations."
        },
        {
          "label": "Private + Unlisted + Public",
          "description": "Three-state visibility. Unlisted = anyone with the URL can view but not listed in any browse page. Public = appears in browse. Slightly more flexible but more UX surface."
        },
        {
          "label": "Private + Public, no browse page",
          "description": "Two-state, but public collections are only accessible via direct link (no /collections browse page). Lighter scope; can add browse later."
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "question": "Should the public registry API (/api/v1/) expose public collections for the CLI to install in one shot?",
      "header": "CLI scope",
      "multiSelect": false,
      "options": [
        {
          "label": "Web only this round (Recommended)",
          "description": "Build the web UI + /api/internal/collections routes now. Defer the public /api/v1/collections endpoints and CLI install-collection support to a follow-up so we don't expand the frozen public contract before the UX has shaken out."
        },
        {
          "label": "Include /api/v1 read endpoints",
          "description": "Add GET /api/v1/collections/[id] alongside the web work so the CLI (and any external integrator) can fetch a public collection's items immediately. No 'install-collection' CLI command yet, just read."
        },
        {
          "label": "Full stack incl. CLI command",
          "description": "Web + /api/v1 + ship a new 'agentcenter install-collection <url>' CLI command in the same change. Largest scope; useful if collection-as-install is the headline feature."
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "question": "What should the URL for a public collection look like?",
      "header": "URL shape",
      "multiSelect": false,
      "options": [
        {
          "label": "/collections/[shortcode] (Recommended)",
          "description": "A short random code (e.g. 10-char base36) generated on creation. Stable, copy-pasteable, no username dependency, works for both private (owner-only access) and public collections via the same route."
        },
        {
          "label": "/collections/[owner]/[slug]",
          "description": "Per-user slug (e.g. /collections/huziyong/my-favorites). Pretty, but requires a stable username on the users table (currently only email + display name exist), and uniqueness scoping logic."
        },
        {
          "label": "/collections/[uuid]",
          "description": "Raw collection UUID in the URL. Simplest, no extra columns or generation logic, but ugly to share."
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/let-s-try-to-plan-velvety-starfish.md",
  "content": "# Collections feature — design & implementation plan\n\n## Context\n\nUsers currently have one implicit bookmark bucket — the `systemKind='saved'` collection — exposed via the bookmark button on extension detail pages and surfaced in Profile → \"Saved\". The schema (`shared/db/schema/collection.ts`) and `docs/plan.md` already anticipate user-created collections (P0 page `/[locale]/collections`, P1 detail page `/[locale]/collections/[id]`), but the feature was never wired up. This change finishes the design:\n\n- Lets users create named collections (with optional bilingual name + description), add/remove extensions, and toggle each collection between **private** and **public**.\n- Public collections get a shareable, stable, copy-pasteable URL (`/collections/<shortcode>`) and appear on a `/collections` browse page.\n- The existing bookmark button becomes a small picker so one-tap \"Save\" still works while explicit named-collection placement is one extra click away.\n- Web scope only this round. The `/api/v1/` public registry contract is **not** touched — CLI install-collection support is deferred so the UX can shake out first.\n\nOutcome: users have a real \"save and organize\" surface in the marketplace, and can share curated bundles by URL.\n\n## Pivotal decisions (taken with the user)\n\n1. **Bookmark UX** — Click opens a small popover with a checkbox list of the user's collections + \"New collection…\" inline. The system **Saved** collection is pinned at the top of the list so one-tap save still works.\n2. **Visibility model** — Two-state enum: `private` | `public`. A `/collections` browse page lists public collections; direct link works for both.\n3. **API scope** — Internal routes under `server/api/internal/collections/...` only. No `/api/v1/` collection endpoints, no new CLI command. Defer.\n4. **URL shape** — `/collections/<shortcode>` where `shortcode` is a 10-character base36 string generated on insert. Same path for private (owner-only access) and public.\n\n## Schema changes\n\nFile: `shared/db/schema/collection.ts`\n\nAdd columns to `collections`:\n\n| Column | Type | Notes |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `slug` | `text not null unique` | 10-char base36 shortcode, generated server-side |\n| `description` | `text` (nullable) | English description, max 500 |\n| `descriptionZh` | `text` (nullable) | Chinese description, max 500 |\n| `visibility` | `collection_visibility` enum (`private`, `public`) `not null default 'private'` | New enum |\n| `publishedAt` | `timestamp({ withTimezone: true })` (nullable) | Set the first time `visibility` flips to `public`; used for browse sort |\n| `updatedAt` | `timestamp({ withTimezone: true }) not null default now()` | Bumped on item add/remove or metadata edit; matches the pattern in `extensions` |\n\nNew enum at top of file: `export const collectionVisibilityEnum = pgEnum(\"collection_visibility\", [\"private\", \"public\"])`.\n\nIndexes:\n\n- `idx_collections_owner_user` on `(ownerUserId)`\n- `idx_collections_visibility_published` on `(visibility, publishedAt DESC)` — drives browse query\n\nSystem collections (`systemKind in ('installed','saved')`) keep `visibility='private'` and are excluded from browse. They get a deterministic auto-generated shortcode like any other row; the difference is purely how the UI treats them.\n\nMigration: generate via `bun run db:generate` (creates a new file under `drizzle/`). A small backfill is needed for existing rows — see \"Backfill\" below.\n\n## Backfill\n\nA one-shot SQL block inside the new migration (so it runs alongside the schema change):\n\n```sql\n-- 1. add new columns + enum (drizzle-kit emits this)\n-- 2. backfill slugs for existing rows\nupdate collections\nset slug = lower(substring(md5(id || random()::text) for 10))\nwhere slug is null;\n-- 3. add the NOT NULL + UNIQUE constraints last\nalter table collections alter column slug set not null;\nalter table collections add constraint collections_slug_unique unique (slug);\n```\n\nCollisions during backfill are vanishingly unlikely (~36^10 keyspace, hundreds of rows), but if drizzle-kit's emitted statement order isn't friendly we may need a hand-written migration in `drizzle/`. Verify by running `bun run db:generate` and reading the diff before applying.\n\n## Shared validators\n\nNew file: `shared/validators/collection.ts`\n\nMirrors the style of `shared/validators/profile.ts`:\n\n```ts\nexport const COLLECTION_NAME_MIN = 1\nexport const COLLECTION_NAME_MAX = 100\nexport const COLLECTION_DESC_MAX = 500\n\nexport const CollectionVisibility = z.enum([\"private\", \"public\"])\n\nexport const CreateCollectionInput = z.object({\n  name: z.string().min(COLLECTION_NAME_MIN).max(COLLECTION_NAME_MAX),\n  nameZh: z.string().max(COLLECTION_NAME_MAX).optional(),\n  description: z.string().max(COLLECTION_DESC_MAX).optional(),\n  descriptionZh: z.string().max(COLLECTION_DESC_MAX).optional(),\n  visibility: CollectionVisibility.default(\"private\"),\n})\n\nexport const UpdateCollectionInput = CreateCollectionInput.partial()\nexport const AddItemInput = z.object({ extensionId: z.string().min(1) })\n```\n\nExport inferred types alongside (`CreateCollectionInput`, etc.) — same pattern the rest of the repo uses.\n\n## Server: shortcode + queries\n\n### `server/utils/collections/shortcode.ts` (new)\n\nSingle helper `generateShortcode(): string` returning 10 base36 chars from `crypto.randomBytes` (Bun-compatible). Routes that insert call a small wrapper `generateUniqueShortcode(db)` which retries up to 5 times on unique-constraint violation before failing — pragmatic, matches the patterns in the existing publish code.\n\n### `server/utils/queries/collections.ts` (new)\n\nFunctions (all parameterized by `db = useDb()`):\n\n- `listForOwner(userId)` → `Array<{ id, slug, name, nameZh, description, descriptionZh, visibility, systemKind, itemCount, updatedAt }>` — owner's collections, includes the `saved` system row tagged via `systemKind`, **excludes** `installed`. Item counts via a single grouped subquery on `collection_items`.\n- `getBySlug(slug, viewerUserId | null)` → collection + items (paginated) + owner display name. Throws `not_found` when row missing; throws `forbidden` when row is `private` and viewer ≠ owner.\n- `listPublicPaged({ page, pageSize })` → public browse list, ordered by `publishedAt DESC`, joined to user for owner display name and aggregate item count.\n- `getMembershipForExtension(extensionId, userId)` → `Set<string>` of collection IDs that already contain this extension — feeds the popover checkbox state.\n- `create({ ownerUserId, ...input })`, `update(slug, ownerUserId, input)`, `remove(slug, ownerUserId)`, `addItem(slug, ownerUserId, extensionId)`, `removeItem(slug, ownerUserId, extensionId)` — `remove*` reject system collections (`systemKind != null`) with a typed `system_collection_locked` error.\n\n`update` is responsible for setting `publishedAt` on the first private→public transition (and leaving it on the row when toggled back to private — keeps \"ever-published\" sort stable; can revisit if it matters).\n\nBoth `addItem` and `removeItem` also bump `updatedAt` on the parent collection.\n\nA small `CollectionError` class (akin to `InstallError` in `shared/installs/record.ts`) carries `code` strings: `not_found`, `forbidden`, `system_collection_locked`, `slug_taken`, `name_required`. Server handlers map codes → HTTP status.\n\n### `server/utils/queries/profile.ts` (existing — extend)\n\nAdd `getCollectionsForUser(userId): Promise<ProfileCollectionRow[]>` returning the same shape as `listForOwner` minus item rows. Wire it into `server/api/internal/profile/section.get.ts` under a new `\"collections\"` case and into the `SectionKey` union + `SectionResponse`.\n\n## Server: API routes\n\nAll under `server/api/internal/collections/`. All use `requireUser(event)` and Zod-validated bodies via `readValidatedBody`. Response shape mirrors the existing `installs.post.ts` convention: `{ ok: true, ...payload }` on success, `createError({ statusCode, statusMessage })` on failure.\n\n| Route | Method | Purpose |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `index.get.ts` | GET | List the current user's collections (incl. `saved`, excl. `installed`). |\n| `index.post.ts` | POST | Create a new collection. Body: `CreateCollectionInput`. Returns `{ ok, slug }`. |\n| `[slug].get.ts` | GET | Fetch a single collection. Owner sees private rows; visitors see public only. Includes paginated items. |\n| `[slug].patch.ts` | PATCH | Update name / description / visibility. Body: `UpdateCollectionInput`. Owner-only. Rejects system collections. |\n| `[slug].delete.ts` | DELETE | Delete a collection. Owner-only. Rejects system collections. |\n| `[slug]/items.post.ts` | POST | Add an extension. Body: `AddItemInput`. Owner-only. Idempotent (PK ignore-on-conflict). |\n| `[slug]/items/[extensionId].delete.ts` | DELETE | Remove an extension. Owner-only. |\n| `public.get.ts` | GET | Paginated public collections for the browse page. Query: `?page=1&pageSize=24`. |\n| `for-extension.get.ts` | GET | Membership lookup. Query: `?extensionId=...`. Returns `{ collectionIds: string[] }`. Powers the picker checkboxes. |\n\nNote: `for-extension.get.ts` is intentionally **not** under `[slug]/` — it's a per-extension query keyed by user, not by collection.\n\nSaved collection lazy-init: `getOrCreateSavedCollection(userId)` already exists implicitly inside `recordInstall` (see `shared/installs/record.ts`). Extract it into `server/utils/queries/collections.ts` so `index.get.ts` and the items routes can lazy-create the \"Saved\" row on first access — keeps existing `recordInstall` behavior intact (it will import the same helper rather than inline-create).\n\n## UI changes\n\n### Bookmark picker — `app/components/extension/SaveToCollectionPopover.vue` (new)\n\nReplaces the inline action in `SaveButton.vue`. Behavior:\n\n1. On first click, fetch `/api/internal/collections` + `/api/internal/collections/for-extension?extensionId=…` in parallel (cached in-memory for the popover's lifetime). Unauthenticated → redirect to `/sign-in` (mirrors current `SaveButton` behavior).\n2. Render a popover anchored to the bookmark icon:\n   - Pinned row at top: **Saved** (system collection) with a checkbox. Toggling POSTs/DELETEs against `/api/internal/collections/<saved-slug>/items`.\n   - List of user-created collections with checkboxes. Each toggle is an immediate POST/DELETE.\n   - Footer: an inline \"+ New collection\" input that creates and adds in one shot (POST `/api/internal/collections`, then POST `/items`).\n3. The bookmark icon's filled-state reflects whether the extension is in **any** collection (membership set non-empty).\n\nThe popover uses Reka UI's `Popover` (already a project dep through shadcn-vue). Localize labels via `useI18n()`.\n\n### `SaveButton.vue` (existing — replace internals)\n\nBecomes a thin wrapper that opens `SaveToCollectionPopover`. Keeps the same props/markup contract so callers in `ExtHero` don't change. The 3-second \"Saved\" transient is replaced by the more honest \"is it in any collection\" indicator.\n\n### Profile section — `app/components/profile/SectionCollections.vue` (new) + `SectionRail.vue` (extend)\n\nAdd `\"collections\"` to the section enum in `SectionRail.vue`, `server/api/internal/profile/section.get.ts`, and `shared/types`. The new component lists user collections in cards: name (bilingual), item count, visibility chip (private/public), updated-at. Each card links to `/collections/<slug>`. Inline actions: rename, delete, toggle visibility, copy public link.\n\nRename/move existing `SectionSaved.vue`? Keep it for now under a separate `\"saved\"` tab — many users have built up muscle memory. Mark in a follow-up issue whether to fold it into `SectionCollections.vue` once the new feature lands.\n\n### Pages\n\n- `app/pages/collections/index.vue` (new) — Public browse. Fetches `/api/internal/collections/public`. Grid of `CollectionCard` (new shared component under `app/components/collection/`). Empty state when no public collections exist. Locale-aware via `useLocalePath`. Pagination via query string.\n- `app/pages/collections/[slug].vue` (new) — Detail. SSR-fetches the collection. Renders header (name, description, owner, visibility chip), item grid using existing extension-card primitives (reuse from `SectionSaved.vue` or `app/components/extension/ExtensionCard.vue` if it exists), and an owner-only edit/delete/visibility-toggle bar. Toggling visibility makes a PATCH; toggling shows a \"Copy link\" affordance once public.\n\n### Navigation\n\nAdd a \"Collections\" link to the TopBar (between Extensions and the user menu) pointing at `/collections`. Hidden when logged out? No — collections browse is public.\n\n### i18n\n\nExtend `i18n/locales/en.json` and `i18n/locales/zh.json` under existing namespaces:\n\n- `profile.sections.collections` — sidebar label.\n- `profile.emptyCollections.{title,body}` — empty state.\n- `collections.browse.{title,subtitle,empty}` — `/collections` page copy.\n- `collections.detail.{visibilityPrivate,visibilityPublic,owner,itemsHeading,empty,copyLink,linkCopied,confirmDelete}`\n- `collections.actions.{create,rename,delete,makePublic,makePrivate,save,cancel}`\n- `collections.form.{namePlaceholder,nameZhPlaceholder,descriptionPlaceholder,descriptionZhPlaceholder,visibilityHelp}`\n- `common.saveToCollection`, `common.newCollection`, `common.added`, `common.removed`\n\nChinese translations included in the same commits.\n\n## Files to create\n\n```\nshared/db/schema/collection.ts                            # extend\nshared/validators/collection.ts                           # new\nshared/types.ts                                           # extend (ProfileCollectionRow, CollectionVisibility)\nserver/utils/collections/shortcode.ts                     # new\nserver/utils/queries/collections.ts                       # new\nserver/utils/queries/profile.ts                           # extend (getCollectionsForUser)\nserver/api/internal/collections/index.get.ts              # new\nserver/api/internal/collections/index.post.ts             # new\nserver/api/internal/collections/[slug].get.ts             # new\nserver/api/internal/collections/[slug].patch.ts           # new\nserver/api/internal/collections/[slug].delete.ts          # new\nserver/api/internal/collections/[slug]/items.post.ts      # new\nserver/api/internal/collections/[slug]/items/[extensionId].delete.ts  # new\nserver/api/internal/collections/public.get.ts             # new\nserver/api/internal/collections/for-extension.get.ts      # new\nserver/api/internal/profile/section.get.ts                # extend (collections case)\napp/pages/collections/index.vue                           # new\napp/pages/collections/[slug].vue                          # new\napp/components/extension/SaveToCollectionPopover.vue      # new\napp/components/extension/SaveButton.vue                   # replace internals, keep props\napp/components/collection/CollectionCard.vue              # new\napp/components/collection/CollectionForm.vue              # new (used in create/rename modal)\napp/components/collection/VisibilityToggle.vue            # new\napp/components/profile/SectionCollections.vue             # new\napp/components/profile/SectionRail.vue                    # extend\napp/components/layout/TopBar.vue                          # extend (nav link)\ni18n/locales/en.json                                      # extend\ni18n/locales/zh.json                                      # extend\ndrizzle/<next>_collections_v2.sql                         # generated + backfill\n```\n\n## Reuse existing code\n\n- `requireUser(event)` from `server/utils/auth.ts` for all internal routes.\n- `useDb()` from `server/utils/db.ts` for the Drizzle handle.\n- `recordInstall` orchestration in `shared/installs/record.ts` — extract its inline `getOrCreateSavedCollection` and reuse from the new collections queries. Keep `recordInstall` semantics unchanged.\n- `getSavedForUser` in `server/utils/queries/profile.ts` — stays; the new `getCollectionsForUser` is additive.\n- `app/components/profile/SectionSaved.vue` row layout — copy the card structure into `CollectionCard.vue` and `SectionCollections.vue`.\n- Zod validators in `shared/validators/profile.ts` for shape, naming, and `_MAX` constant style.\n- Reka UI `Popover` primitive — already wired through shadcn-vue (`app/components/ui/`) — use for the picker.\n\n## Out of scope (deferred)\n\n- No `/api/v1/collections/*` public registry endpoints. CLI cannot install-by-collection.\n- No collection-level ratings, comments, or fork/clone.\n- No org-scoped or department-scoped collections (only user-owned). Multi-tenancy hook exists on `collections.ownerUserId`; org ownership can be added later by introducing `ownerOrgId`.\n- No featured/curated collections on the home page. The home featured hero stays as-is.\n- No `unlisted` third visibility state — can be added later by extending the enum without breaking rows.\n\n## Verification\n\nEnd-to-end manual flow (run `bun run dev`, sign in as a test user):\n\n1. **Schema** — `bun run db:generate` produces a clean migration; `bun run db:migrate` applies it; existing `saved` rows now have non-null `slug`, `visibility='private'`, item counts unchanged.\n2. **Bookmark picker** — Open any extension detail page. Click bookmark → popover shows \"Saved\" pinned at top. Tick → row appears in Profile → Saved. Untick → row disappears.\n3. **Create collection** — In the popover, \"+ New collection\" → name \"Test Pack\" → it gains a checkbox, ticking adds the current extension.\n4. **Profile section** — Profile → Collections shows \"Test Pack\" (1 item) and \"Saved\" tagged as system.\n5. **Detail page** — Click \"Test Pack\" card → lands on `/collections/<shortcode>` with the extension listed. Owner-only edit bar visible.\n6. **Visibility toggle** — Toggle Test Pack to Public. URL stays the same. `publishedAt` populated. Open in a private window (signed out) → page loads, no edit bar, item visible.\n7. **Browse** — Sign out, visit `/collections` → \"Test Pack\" appears in the public browse grid with owner name.\n8. **Delete** — Back as owner, delete Test Pack → confirms, removes from profile + browse. Confirm `collection_items` cascade fired (no orphans).\n9. **System collection guard** — Try `DELETE /api/internal/collections/<saved-slug>` directly via devtools → 422 with `system_collection_locked`.\n10. **i18n** — Switch locale via TopBar. Every new string renders in zh; nothing falls back to a missing-key marker.\n\nAutomated coverage (added in the same PR series):\n\n- `shared/validators/collection.test.ts` — Zod input cases (length bounds, visibility enum).\n- `server/utils/queries/collections.test.ts` — integration test against the test DB: create + add + visibility toggle + browse + delete + system-collection guard.\n- `shared/installs/record.test.ts` — keep green; confirms the extract-and-reuse of `getOrCreateSavedCollection` didn't regress install events.\n\nPhase/commit slicing (per `docs/plan.md` §13):\n\n1. `feat(p2-db): extend collections schema with slug + visibility + description` — schema + migration + backfill.\n2. `feat(p9-collections): collections query layer + shortcode helper` — `server/utils/queries/collections.ts`, `shortcode.ts`, extract `getOrCreateSavedCollection`.\n3. `feat(p9-collections): internal collections API routes` — all `server/api/internal/collections/*` routes + validators.\n4. `feat(p9-collections): save-to-collection popover + SaveButton rewire` — UI for picker + popover.\n5. `feat(p9-collections): /collections browse + detail pages` — public-facing pages + nav link.\n6. `feat(p9-collections): profile collections section` — `SectionCollections` + section.get.ts wiring.\n7. `feat(i18n): collections strings (en + zh)` — locale bundles.\n\nEach step ends green on the validate pipeline before the next begins.\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Collections feature — design & implementation plan

## Context

Users currently have one implicit bookmark bucket — the `systemKind='saved'` collection — exposed via the bookmark button on extension detail pages and surfaced in Profile → "Saved". The schema (`shared/db/schema/collection.ts`) and `docs/plan.md` already anticipate user-created collections (P0 page `/[locale]/collections`, P1 detail page `/[locale]/collections/[id]`), but the feature was never wired up. This change finishes the design:

- Lets users create named collections (with optional bilingual name + description), add/remove extensions, and toggle each collection between **private** and **public**.
- Public collections get a shareable, stable, copy-pasteable URL (`/collections/<shortcode>`) and appear on a `/collections` browse page.
- The existing bookmark button becomes a small picker so one-tap "Save" still works while explicit named-collection placement is one extra click away.
- Web scope only this round. The `/api/v1/` public registry contract is **not** touched — CLI install-collection support is deferred so the UX can shake out first.

Outcome: users have a real "save and organize" surface in the marketplace, and can share curated bundles by URL.

## Pivotal decisions (taken with the user)

1. **Bookmark UX** — Click opens a small popover with a checkbox list of the user's collections + "New collection…" inline. The system **Saved** collection is pinned at the top of the list so one-tap save still works.
2. **Visibility model** — Two-state enum: `private` | `public`. A `/collections` browse page lists public collections; direct link works for both.
3. **API scope** — Internal routes under `server/api/internal/collections/...` only. No `/api/v1/` collection endpoints, no new CLI command. Defer.
4. **URL shape** — `/collections/<shortcode>` where `shortcode` is a 10-character base36 string generated on insert. Same path for private (owner-only access) and public.

## Schema changes

File: `shared/db/schema/collection.ts`

Add columns to `collections`:

| Column | Type | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `slug` | `text not null unique` | 10-char base36 shortcode, generated server-side |
| `description` | `text` (nullable) | English description, max 500 |
| `descriptionZh` | `text` (nullable) | Chinese description, max 500 |
| `visibility` | `collection_visibility` enum (`private`, `public`) `not null default 'private'` | New enum |
| `publishedAt` | `timestamp({ withTimezone: true })` (nullable) | Set the first time `visibility` flips to `public`; used for browse sort |
| `updatedAt` | `timestamp({ withTimezone: true }) not null default now()` | Bumped on item add/remove or metadata edit; matches the pattern in `extensions` |

New enum at top of file: `export const collectionVisibilityEnum = pgEnum("collection_visibility", ["private", "public"])`.

Indexes:

- `idx_collections_owner_user` on `(ownerUserId)`
- `idx_collections_visibility_published` on `(visibility, publishedAt DESC)` — drives browse query

System collections (`systemKind in ('installed','saved')`) keep `visibility='private'` and are excluded from browse. They get a deterministic auto-generated shortcode like any other row; the difference is purely how the UI treats them.

Migration: generate via `bun run db:generate` (creates a new file under `drizzle/`). A small backfill is needed for existing rows — see "Backfill" below.

## Backfill

A one-shot SQL block inside the new migration (so it runs alongside the schema change):

```sql
-- 1. add new columns + enum (drizzle-kit emits this)
-- 2. backfill slugs for existing rows
update collections
set slug = lower(substring(md5(id || random()::text) for 10))
where slug is null;
-- 3. add the NOT NULL + UNIQUE constraints last
alter table collections alter column slug set not null;
alter table collections add constraint collections_slug_unique unique (slug);
```

Collisions during backfill are vanishingly unlikely (~36^10 keyspace, hundreds of rows), but if drizzle-kit's emitted statement order isn't friendly we may need a hand-written migration in `drizzle/`. Verify by running `bun run db:generate` and reading the diff before applying.

## Shared validators

New file: `shared/validators/collection.ts`

Mirrors the style of `shared/validators/profile.ts`:

```ts
export const COLLECTION_NAME_MIN = 1
export const COLLECTION_NAME_MAX = 100
export const COLLECTION_DESC_MAX = 500

export const CollectionVisibility = z.enum(["private", "public"])

export const CreateCollectionInput = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(COLLECTION_NAME_MIN).max(COLLECTION_NAME_MAX),
  nameZh: z.string().max(COLLECTION_NAME_MAX).optional(),
  description: z.string().max(COLLECTION_DESC_MAX).optional(),
  descriptionZh: z.string().max(COLLECTION_DESC_MAX).optional(),
  visibility: CollectionVisibility.default("private"),
})

export const UpdateCollectionInput = CreateCollectionInput.partial()
export const AddItemInput = z.object({ extensionId: z.string().min(1) })
```

Export inferred types alongside (`CreateCollectionInput`, etc.) — same pattern the rest of the repo uses.

## Server: shortcode + queries

### `server/utils/collections/shortcode.ts` (new)

Single helper `generateShortcode(): string` returning 10 base36 chars from `crypto.randomBytes` (Bun-compatible). Routes that insert call a small wrapper `generateUniqueShortcode(db)` which retries up to 5 times on unique-constraint violation before failing — pragmatic, matches the patterns in the existing publish code.

### `server/utils/queries/collections.ts` (new)

Functions (all parameterized by `db = useDb()`):

- `listForOwner(userId)` → `Array<{ id, slug, name, nameZh, description, descriptionZh, visibility, systemKind, itemCount, updatedAt }>` — owner's collections, includes the `saved` system row tagged via `systemKind`, **excludes** `installed`. Item counts via a single grouped subquery on `collection_items`.
- `getBySlug(slug, viewerUserId | null)` → collection + items (paginated) + owner display name. Throws `not_found` when row missing; throws `forbidden` when row is `private` and viewer ≠ owner.
- `listPublicPaged({ page, pageSize })` → public browse list, ordered by `publishedAt DESC`, joined to user for owner display name and aggregate item count.
- `getMembershipForExtension(extensionId, userId)` → `Set<string>` of collection IDs that already contain this extension — feeds the popover checkbox state.
- `create({ ownerUserId, ...input })`, `update(slug, ownerUserId, input)`, `remove(slug, ownerUserId)`, `addItem(slug, ownerUserId, extensionId)`, `removeItem(slug, ownerUserId, extensionId)` — `remove*` reject system collections (`systemKind != null`) with a typed `system_collection_locked` error.

`update` is responsible for setting `publishedAt` on the first private→public transition (and leaving it on the row when toggled back to private — keeps "ever-published" sort stable; can revisit if it matters).

Both `addItem` and `removeItem` also bump `updatedAt` on the parent collection.

A small `CollectionError` class (akin to `InstallError` in `shared/installs/record.ts`) carries `code` strings: `not_found`, `forbidden`, `system_collection_locked`, `slug_taken`, `name_required`. Server handlers map codes → HTTP status.

### `server/utils/queries/profile.ts` (existing — extend)

Add `getCollectionsForUser(userId): Promise<ProfileCollectionRow[]>` returning the same shape as `listForOwner` minus item rows. Wire it into `server/api/internal/profile/section.get.ts` under a new `"collections"` case and into the `SectionKey` union + `SectionResponse`.

## Server: API routes

All under `server/api/internal/collections/`. All use `requireUser(event)` and Zod-validated bodies via `readValidatedBody`. Response shape mirrors the existing `installs.post.ts` convention: `{ ok: true, ...payload }` on success, `createError({ statusCode, statusMessage })` on failure.

| Route | Method | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `index.get.ts` | GET | List the current user's collections (incl. `saved`, excl. `installed`). |
| `index.post.ts` | POST | Create a new collection. Body: `CreateCollectionInput`. Returns `{ ok, slug }`. |
| `[slug].get.ts` | GET | Fetch a single collection. Owner sees private rows; visitors see public only. Includes paginated items. |
| `[slug].patch.ts` | PATCH | Update name / description / visibility. Body: `UpdateCollectionInput`. Owner-only. Rejects system collections. |
| `[slug].delete.ts` | DELETE | Delete a collection. Owner-only. Rejects system collections. |
| `[slug]/items.post.ts` | POST | Add an extension. Body: `AddItemInput`. Owner-only. Idempotent (PK ignore-on-conflict). |
| `[slug]/items/[extensionId].delete.ts` | DELETE | Remove an extension. Owner-only. |
| `public.get.ts` | GET | Paginated public collections for the browse page. Query: `?page=1&pageSize=24`. |
| `for-extension.get.ts` | GET | Membership lookup. Query: `?extensionId=...`. Returns `{ collectionIds: string[] }`. Powers the picker checkboxes. |

Note: `for-extension.get.ts` is intentionally **not** under `[slug]/` — it's a per-extension query keyed by user, not by collection.

Saved collection lazy-init: `getOrCreateSavedCollection(userId)` already exists implicitly inside `recordInstall` (see `shared/installs/record.ts`). Extract it into `server/utils/queries/collections.ts` so `index.get.ts` and the items routes can lazy-create the "Saved" row on first access — keeps existing `recordInstall` behavior intact (it will import the same helper rather than inline-create).

## UI changes

### Bookmark picker — `app/components/extension/SaveToCollectionPopover.vue` (new)

Replaces the inline action in `SaveButton.vue`. Behavior:

1. On first click, fetch `/api/internal/collections` + `/api/internal/collections/for-extension?extensionId=…` in parallel (cached in-memory for the popover's lifetime). Unauthenticated → redirect to `/sign-in` (mirrors current `SaveButton` behavior).
2. Render a popover anchored to the bookmark icon:
   - Pinned row at top: **Saved** (system collection) with a checkbox. Toggling POSTs/DELETEs against `/api/internal/collections/<saved-slug>/items`.
   - List of user-created collections with checkboxes. Each toggle is an immediate POST/DELETE.
   - Footer: an inline "+ New collection" input that creates and adds in one shot (POST `/api/internal/collections`, then POST `/items`).
3. The bookmark icon's filled-state reflects whether the extension is in **any** collection (membership set non-empty).

The popover uses Reka UI's `Popover` (already a project dep through shadcn-vue). Localize labels via `useI18n()`.

### `SaveButton.vue` (existing — replace internals)

Becomes a thin wrapper that opens `SaveToCollectionPopover`. Keeps the same props/markup contract so callers in `ExtHero` don't change. The 3-second "Saved" transient is replaced by the more honest "is it in any collection" indicator.

### Profile section — `app/components/profile/SectionCollections.vue` (new) + `SectionRail.vue` (extend)

Add `"collections"` to the section enum in `SectionRail.vue`, `server/api/internal/profile/section.get.ts`, and `shared/types`. The new component lists user collections in cards: name (bilingual), item count, visibility chip (private/public), updated-at. Each card links to `/collections/<slug>`. Inline actions: rename, delete, toggle visibility, copy public link.

Rename/move existing `SectionSaved.vue`? Keep it for now under a separate `"saved"` tab — many users have built up muscle memory. Mark in a follow-up issue whether to fold it into `SectionCollections.vue` once the new feature lands.

### Pages

- `app/pages/collections/index.vue` (new) — Public browse. Fetches `/api/internal/collections/public`. Grid of `CollectionCard` (new shared component under `app/components/collection/`). Empty state when no public collections exist. Locale-aware via `useLocalePath`. Pagination via query string.
- `app/pages/collections/[slug].vue` (new) — Detail. SSR-fetches the collection. Renders header (name, description, owner, visibility chip), item grid using existing extension-card primitives (reuse from `SectionSaved.vue` or `app/components/extension/ExtensionCard.vue` if it exists), and an owner-only edit/delete/visibility-toggle bar. Toggling visibility makes a PATCH; toggling shows a "Copy link" affordance once public.

### Navigation

Add a "Collections" link to the TopBar (between Extensions and the user menu) pointing at `/collections`. Hidden when logged out? No — collections browse is public.

### i18n

Extend `i18n/locales/en.json` and `i18n/locales/zh.json` under existing namespaces:

- `profile.sections.collections` — sidebar label.
- `profile.emptyCollections.{title,body}` — empty state.
- `collections.browse.{title,subtitle,empty}` — `/collections` page copy.
- `collections.detail.{visibilityPrivate,visibilityPublic,owner,itemsHeading,empty,copyLink,linkCopied,confirmDelete}`
- `collections.actions.{create,rename,delete,makePublic,makePrivate,save,cancel}`
- `collections.form.{namePlaceholder,nameZhPlaceholder,descriptionPlaceholder,descriptionZhPlaceholder,visibilityHelp}`
- `common.saveToCollection`, `common.newCollection`, `common.added`, `common.removed`

Chinese translations included in the same commits.

## Reuse existing code

- `requireUser(event)` from `server/utils/auth.ts` for all internal routes.
- `useDb()` from `server/utils/db.ts` for the Drizzle handle.
- `recordInstall` orchestration in `shared/installs/record.ts` — extract its inline `getOrCreateSavedCollection` and reuse from the new collections queries. Keep `recordInstall` semantics unchanged.
- `getSavedForUser` in `server/utils/queries/profile.ts` — stays; the new `getCollectionsForUser` is additive.
- `app/components/profile/SectionSaved.vue` row layout — copy the card structure into `CollectionCard.vue` and `SectionCollections.vue`.
- Zod validators in `shared/validators/profile.ts` for shape, naming, and `_MAX` constant style.
- Reka UI `Popover` primitive — already wired through shadcn-vue (`app/components/ui/`) — use for the picker.

## Out of scope (deferred)

- No `/api/v1/collections/*` public registry endpoints. CLI cannot install-by-collection.
- No collection-level ratings, comments, or fork/clone.
- No org-scoped or department-scoped collections (only user-owned). Multi-tenancy hook exists on `collections.ownerUserId`; org ownership can be added later by introducing `ownerOrgId`.
- No featured/curated collections on the home page. The home featured hero stays as-is.
- No `unlisted` third visibility state — can be added later by extending the enum without breaking rows.

## Verification

End-to-end manual flow (run `bun run dev`, sign in as a test user):

1. **Schema** — `bun run db:generate` produces a clean migration; `bun run db:migrate` applies it; existing `saved` rows now have non-null `slug`, `visibility='private'`, item counts unchanged.
2. **Bookmark picker** — Open any extension detail page. Click bookmark → popover shows "Saved" pinned at top. Tick → row appears in Profile → Saved. Untick → row disappears.
3. **Create collection** — In the popover, "+ New collection" → name "Test Pack" → it gains a checkbox, ticking adds the current extension.
4. **Profile section** — Profile → Collections shows "Test Pack" (1 item) and "Saved" tagged as system.
5. **Detail page** — Click "Test Pack" card → lands on `/collections/<shortcode>` with the extension listed. Owner-only edit bar visible.
6. **Visibility toggle** — Toggle Test Pack to Public. URL stays the same. `publishedAt` populated. Open in a private window (signed out) → page loads, no edit bar, item visible.
7. **Browse** — Sign out, visit `/collections` → "Test Pack" appears in the public browse grid with owner name.
8. **Delete** — Back as owner, delete Test Pack → confirms, removes from profile + browse. Confirm `collection_items` cascade fired (no orphans).
9. **System collection guard** — Try `DELETE /api/internal/collections/<saved-slug>` directly via devtools → 422 with `system_collection_locked`.
10. **i18n** — Switch locale via TopBar. Every new string renders in zh; nothing falls back to a missing-key marker.

Automated coverage (added in the same PR series):

- `shared/validators/collection.test.ts` — Zod input cases (length bounds, visibility enum).
- `server/utils/queries/collections.test.ts` — integration test against the test DB: create + add + visibility toggle + browse + delete + system-collection guard.
- `shared/installs/record.test.ts` — keep green; confirms the extract-and-reuse of `getOrCreateSavedCollection` didn't regress install events.

Phase/commit slicing (per `docs/plan.md` §13):

1. `feat(p2-db): extend collections schema with slug + visibility + description` — schema + migration + backfill.
2. `feat(p9-collections): collections query layer + shortcode helper` — `server/utils/queries/collections.ts`, `shortcode.ts`, extract `getOrCreateSavedCollection`.
3. `feat(p9-collections): internal collections API routes` — all `server/api/internal/collections/*` routes + validators.
4. `feat(p9-collections): save-to-collection popover + SaveButton rewire` — UI for picker + popover.
5. `feat(p9-collections): /collections browse + detail pages` — public-facing pages + nav link.
6. `feat(p9-collections): profile collections section` — `SectionCollections` + section.get.ts wiring.
7. `feat(i18n): collections strings (en + zh)` — locale bundles.

Each step ends green on the validate pipeline before the next begins.

```
1	// Pure decision for the install flow's "which version to record" step.
2	// Same testability story as `shared/extensions/state.ts` — the orchestrator
3	// loads the candidate list and passes it in; this module just decides.
4	
5	export interface VersionCandidate {
6	  version: string
7	  publishedAt: Date | null
8	  createdAt: Date
9	}
10	
11	export type VersionPick =
12	  | { ok: true; version: string }
13	  | { ok: false; error: "no_published_version" }
14	
15	// `candidates` must already be sorted newest-first
16	// (`publishedAt DESC NULLS LAST, createdAt DESC` — matches what
17	// `versionsRepo.listLatestReadyForExtension` returns).
18	//
19	// - If the caller passed a specific version, that wins (passthrough).
20	// - Otherwise pick the first candidate; if there is none, return an error
21	//   so the orchestrator can map it to its domain exception.
22	export function pickInstallVersion(input: {
23	  requested: string | undefined
24	  candidates: VersionCandidate[]
25	}): VersionPick {
26	  const { requested, candidates } = input
27	
28	  if (requested) return { ok: true, version: requested }
29	
30	  const latest = candidates[0]
31	  if (!latest) return { ok: false, error: "no_published_version" }
32	
33	  return { ok: true, version: latest.version }
34	}
35	
```

70	]);

72	export const extensions = pgTable(
73	  "extensions",
74	  {
75	    id: text().primaryKey(),
76	    slug: text().notNull().unique(),
77	    category: extensionCategoryEnum().notNull(),
78	    badge: extensionBadgeEnum(),

82	    funcCat: funcCatEnum(),
83	    subCat: text(),
84	    l2: text(),
85	    publisherUserId: text().references(() => users.id, {
86	      onDelete: "set null",
87	    }),
88	    ownerOrgId: text()
89	      .notNull()
90	      .references(() => organizations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
91	    deptId: text().references(() => departments.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
92	    iconEmoji: text(),
93	    iconColor: text(),
94	    visibility: visibilityEnum().notNull().default("draft"),
95	    // Editorial flag — hand-curated. Surfaces a single extension on the home
96	    // hero. Coexists with `badge`: badge is a static editorial mark on the
97	    // card; `featured` is a transient "pick of the week" pointer.
98	    featured: boolean().notNull().default(false),

100	    name: text().notNull(),
101	    nameZh: text(),
102	    tagline: text(),
103	    taglineZh: text(),
104	    description: text(),
105	    descriptionZh: text(),
106	    // Long-form markdown body for the detail page. Not duplicated to ZH yet —
107	    // most extensions will have an English README, with translations layered

109	    readmeMd: text(),

111	    homepageUrl: text(),

113	    licenseSpdx: text(),
114	    compatibilityJson: jsonb(),
115	    // Permissions captured during publish (network/files/runtime/data, etc.).
116	    // Surfaced on the detail page so users see what the extension wants.
117	    permissions: jsonb().notNull().default({}),

129	    index("idx_ext_scope").on(t.scope),

162	    sourceMethod: text().notNull().default("zip"),

1	import * as collectionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/collections"
2	import * as extensionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/extensions"
3	import * as installsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/installs"
4	import * as versionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/versions"
5	import { pickInstallVersion } from "~~/shared/installs/record"
6	
7	import { useDb } from "./db"
8	
9	// Orchestrator over the installs/collections/extensions/versions repos. The
10	// "which version to install" decision is still inline here; commit 8
11	// extracts it into the pure `shared/installs/record.ts` decision module.
12	
13	export type ExtensionRef = { id: string } | { slug: string }
14	export type InstallSource = "web" | "cli"
15	export type InstallErrorCode = "extension_not_found" | "no_published_version"
16	
17	export class InstallError extends Error {
18	  readonly code: InstallErrorCode
19	  constructor(code: InstallErrorCode) {
20	    super(code)
21	    this.code = code
22	    this.name = "InstallError"
23	  }
24	}
25	
26	export interface RecordInstallParams {
27	  userId: string
28	  extension: ExtensionRef
29	  source: InstallSource
30	  version?: string | undefined
31	}
32	
33	export interface InstallRecord {
34	  installId: string
35	  isFirstInstall: boolean
36	  version: string
37	}
38	
39	export async function recordInstall(
40	  params: RecordInstallParams,
41	): Promise<InstallRecord> {
42	  const { userId, extension, source } = params
43	  const db = useDb()
44	
45	  // Resolve the extension. `findBySlug` is `onlyPublished: true` by default,
46	  // which would hide drafts — the install flow is only allowed against
47	  // published extensions anyway, so the default is what we want.
48	  const ext =
49	    "id" in extension
50	      ? await extensionsRepo.findById(db, extension.id)
51	      : await extensionsRepo.findBySlug(db, extension.slug)
52	  if (!ext) throw new InstallError("extension_not_found")
53	  const extensionId = ext.id
54	
55	  // Resolve which version to install. The orchestrator only loads the
56	  // candidate list when it's actually needed (`requested` is undefined).
57	  const candidates = params.version
58	    ? []
59	    : await versionsRepo.listLatestReadyForExtension(db, extensionId)
60	  const pick = pickInstallVersion({ requested: params.version, candidates })
61	  if (!pick.ok) throw new InstallError(pick.error)
62	  const version = pick.version
63	
64	  const { id: installedColId } = await collectionsRepo.getOrCreateSystem(
65	    db,
66	    userId,
67	    "installed",
68	  )
69	  const installId = crypto.randomUUID()
70	  const resolvedVersion = version
71	
72	  const isFirstInstall = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
73	    const prior = await installsRepo.findByUserAndExtension(
74	      tx,
75	      userId,
76	      extensionId,
77	    )
78	    const isFirst = prior === null
79	
80	    await installsRepo.insertInstall(tx, {
81	      id: installId,
82	      userId,
83	      extensionId,
84	      version: resolvedVersion,
85	      source,
86	    })
87	    await collectionsRepo.addItem(tx, installedColId, extensionId)
88	    await extensionsRepo.incrementDownloads(tx, extensionId)
89	
90	    return isFirst
91	  })
92	
93	  return { installId, isFirstInstall, version: resolvedVersion }
94	}
95	
```

1	import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm"
2	
3	import { collectionItems, collections } from "~~/shared/db/schema"
4	
5	import type { Transactable } from "./types"
6	
7	// `collections` + `collection_items` table accessor. The install
8	// orchestrator uses these to make sure every install also drops a row into
9	// the user's "Installed" system collection.
10	
11	export type SystemKind = "installed" | "saved"
12	
13	const SYSTEM_KIND_LABEL: Record<SystemKind, string> = {
14	  installed: "Installed",
15	  saved: "Saved",
16	}
17	
18	// Look up (or create) the per-user system collection of the given kind.
19	// Idempotent: safe to call concurrently — the worst case is two rows of
20	// the same kind for the same user, which the install flow then both
21	// `addItem`s into without breakage. If that race ever shows up in prod,
22	// add a partial unique index on (owner_user_id, system_kind).
23	export async function getOrCreateSystem(
24	  db: Transactable,
25	  userId: string,
26	  kind: SystemKind,
27	): Promise<{ id: string }> {
28	  const existing = await db
29	    .select({ id: collections.id })
30	    .from(collections)
31	    .where(
32	      and(
33	        eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId),
34	        eq(collections.systemKind, kind),
35	      ),
36	    )
37	    .limit(1)
38	  if (existing[0]) return existing[0]
39	
40	  const id = crypto.randomUUID()
41	  await db.insert(collections).values({
42	    id,
43	    ownerUserId: userId,
44	    name: SYSTEM_KIND_LABEL[kind],
45	    systemKind: kind,
46	  })
47	  return { id }
48	}
49	
50	export async function addItem(
51	  db: Transactable,
52	  collectionId: string,
53	  extensionId: string,
54	): Promise<void> {
55	  await db
56	    .insert(collectionItems)
57	    .values({ collectionId, extensionId })
58	    .onConflictDoNothing()
59	}
60	
```

1	import type { ExtractTablesWithRelations } from "drizzle-orm"
2	import type { PgDatabase, PgQueryResultHKT } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core"
3	
4	import type * as schema from "~~/shared/db/schema"
5	
6	type Schema = typeof schema
7	
8	// `Transactable` covers any concrete Drizzle Postgres database — both the
9	// production `postgres-js` driver and the PGlite driver used in integration
10	// tests — *and* an open transaction handle (`PgTransaction` extends
11	// `PgDatabase`). Repository functions accept this and never branch on the
12	// driver, so an orchestrator can pass either `useDb()` or a `tx` callback
13	// argument and the same repo code works for both.
14	export type Transactable = PgDatabase<
15	  PgQueryResultHKT,
16	  Schema,
17	  ExtractTablesWithRelations<Schema>
18	>
19	
```

16	// `extensions` table accessor. Every function takes a `Transactable` as the

18	// PGlite-backed integration test DB.

79	}

81	const relatedSelect = {
82	  id: extensions.id,
83	  slug: extensions.slug,
84	  name: extensions.name,
85	  nameZh: extensions.nameZh,
86	  iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,
87	  iconColor: extensions.iconColor,

94	export async function findManyForList(
95	  db: Transactable,
96	  filters: Filters,
97	  userDeptId?: string,
98	) {
99	  const where = buildExtensionWhere(db, filters, userDeptId)
100	  const order = buildExtensionOrder(filters.sort)
101	
102	  return db
103	    .select(listSelect)
104	    .from(extensions)
105	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
106	    .where(where)
107	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
108	    .orderBy(...order)
109	    .limit(PAGE_SIZE)
110	    .offset(pageOffset(filters.page))
111	}
112	
113	export async function countFiltered(
114	  db: Transactable,
115	  filters: Filters,
116	  userDeptId?: string,
117	): Promise<number> {
118	  const where = buildExtensionWhere(db, filters, userDeptId)
119	  const [row] = await db
120	    .select({ count: count() })
121	    .from(extensions)
122	    .where(where)
123	  return Number(row?.count ?? 0)
124	}
125	
126	export async function findFeatured(db: Transactable) {
127	  // Prefer the hand-curated row. Fall back to top-downloaded so the home
128	  // hero is never empty.
129	  //
130	  // Both queries cap to one row; without a stable tiebreaker the choice
131	  // would flap on ties (two curated rows published at the same instant,
132	  // or two extensions with identical download counts). Sort by id last so
133	  // the home hero is deterministic across requests.
134	  const [curated] = await db
135	    .select(listSelect)
136	    .from(extensions)
137	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
138	    .where(
139	      and(
140	        eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
141	        eq(extensions.featured, true),
142	      ),
143	    )
144	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
145	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.publishedAt), desc(extensions.id))
146	    .limit(1)
147	  if (curated) return curated
148	
149	  const [fallback] = await db
150	    .select(listSelect)
151	    .from(extensions)
152	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
153	    .where(eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))
154	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
155	    .orderBy(
156	      desc(extensions.downloadsCount),
157	      desc(extensions.publishedAt),
158	      desc(extensions.id),
159	    )
160	    .limit(1)
161	  return fallback ?? null
162	}
163	
164	export async function findRelated(
165	  db: Transactable,
166	  extensionId: string,
167	  category: "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins",
168	) {
169	  return db
170	    .select(relatedSelect)
171	    .from(extensions)
172	    .where(
173	      and(
174	        eq(extensions.category, category),
175	        eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
176	        ne(extensions.id, extensionId),
177	      ),
178	    )
179	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))
180	    .limit(4)
181	}
182	
183	export async function findBySlug(
184	  db: Transactable,
185	  slug: string,
186	  opts: { onlyPublished?: boolean } = {},
187	) {
188	  const onlyPublished = opts.onlyPublished ?? true
189	  const where = onlyPublished
190	    ? and(eq(extensions.slug, slug), eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))
191	    : eq(extensions.slug, slug)
192	
193	  const [row] = await db
194	    .select(detailSelect)
195	    .from(extensions)
196	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
197	    .where(where)
198	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
199	  return row ?? null
200	}
201	
202	export async function findById(db: Transactable, id: string) {
203	  const [row] = await db
204	    .select(detailSelect)
205	    .from(extensions)
206	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
207	    .where(eq(extensions.id, id))
208	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
209	  return row ?? null
210	}
211	
212	export interface VisibilityUpdate {
213	  visibility: "draft" | "published" | "archived"
214	  publishedAt: Date | null
215	}
216	
217	export async function updateVisibility(
218	  db: Transactable,
219	  id: string,
220	  payload: VisibilityUpdate,
221	): Promise<void> {
222	  await db.update(extensions).set(payload).where(eq(extensions.id, id))
223	}
224	
225	export async function incrementDownloads(
226	  db: Transactable,
227	  id: string,
228	): Promise<void> {
229	  await db
230	    .update(extensions)
231	    .set({ downloadsCount: sql`${extensions.downloadsCount} + 1` })
232	    .where(eq(extensions.id, id))
233	}
234
