  "content": "import { sql } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport {\n  index,\n  pgEnum,\n  pgTable,\n  primaryKey,\n  text,\n  timestamp,\n} from \"drizzle-orm/pg-core\";\n\nimport { users } from \"./auth\";\nimport { extensions } from \"./extension\";\n\nexport const systemKindEnum = pgEnum(\"system_kind\", [\"installed\", \"saved\"]);\n\nexport const collectionVisibilityEnum = pgEnum(\"collection_visibility\", [\n  \"private\",\n  \"public\",\n]);\n\nexport const collections = pgTable(\n  \"collections\",\n  {\n    id: text().primaryKey(),\n    // 10-char base36 shortcode used in the public URL. Generated server-side\n    // on insert. Kept separate from `id` so the URL space stays stable even\n    // if the internal id ever changes.\n    slug: text().notNull().unique(),\n    ownerUserId: text()\n      .notNull()\n      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n    name: text().notNull(),\n    nameZh: text(),\n    description: text(),\n    descriptionZh: text(),\n    // null for user-created groups; non-null for the auto-managed Installed/Saved\n    systemKind: systemKindEnum(),\n    visibility: collectionVisibilityEnum().notNull().default(\"private\"),\n    // Set the first time `visibility` flips to `public`. Drives browse sort\n    // order. Not reset on a public→private toggle, so \"ever-published\" sort\n    // remains stable; revisit if that causes confusion.\n    publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),\n    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),\n    updatedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),\n  },\n  (t) => [\n    index(\"idx_collections_owner_user\").on(t.ownerUserId),\n    index(\"idx_collections_visibility_published\").on(\n      t.visibility,\n      sql`${t.publishedAt} DESC`,\n    ),\n  ],\n);\n\nexport const collectionItems = pgTable(\n  \"collection_items\",\n  {\n    collectionId: text()\n      .notNull()\n      .references(() => collections.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n    extensionId: text()\n      .notNull()\n      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n    addedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),\n  },\n  (t) => [primaryKey({ columns: [t.collectionId, t.extensionId] })],\n);\n"
}

  "content": "import { z } from \"zod\"\n\n// Input schemas for the collections feature. Mirrors the lightweight,\n// constant-led style used in `profile.ts` so the API handlers can call\n// `.parse(raw)` directly and the UI can import the same `_MAX` constants for\n// inline character counters.\nexport const COLLECTION_NAME_MIN = 1\nexport const COLLECTION_NAME_MAX = 100\nexport const COLLECTION_DESC_MAX = 500\n\nexport const CollectionVisibility = z.enum([\"private\", \"public\"])\nexport type CollectionVisibility = z.infer<typeof CollectionVisibility>\n\nexport const CreateCollectionInput = z.object({\n  name: z.string().trim().min(COLLECTION_NAME_MIN).max(COLLECTION_NAME_MAX),\n  nameZh: z\n    .string()\n    .trim()\n    .max(COLLECTION_NAME_MAX)\n    .optional()\n    .transform((v) => (v && v.length > 0 ? v : undefined)),\n  description: z\n    .string()\n    .trim()\n    .max(COLLECTION_DESC_MAX)\n    .optional()\n    .transform((v) => (v && v.length > 0 ? v : undefined)),\n  descriptionZh: z\n    .string()\n    .trim()\n    .max(COLLECTION_DESC_MAX)\n    .optional()\n    .transform((v) => (v && v.length > 0 ? v : undefined)),\n  visibility: CollectionVisibility.default(\"private\"),\n})\n\nexport type CreateCollectionInput = z.infer<typeof CreateCollectionInput>\n\nexport const UpdateCollectionInput = CreateCollectionInput.partial()\nexport type UpdateCollectionInput = z.infer<typeof UpdateCollectionInput>\n\nexport const AddItemInput = z.object({\n  extensionId: z.string().min(1),\n})\nexport type AddItemInput = z.infer<typeof AddItemInput>\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/utils/collections/shortcode.ts",
  "content": "import { randomBytes } from \"node:crypto\"\n\nimport { eq } from \"drizzle-orm\"\n\nimport type { Transactable } from \"~~/server/repositories/types\"\nimport { collections } from \"~~/shared/db/schema\"\n\n// 10 base36 characters → ~36^10 ≈ 3.6×10^15 codes. At 10^6 rows the\n// birthday-collision probability is still under 10^-3 so a handful of retries\n// is plenty.\nexport const SHORTCODE_LENGTH = 10\nconst ALPHABET = \"REDACTED\"\nconst ALPHABET_LEN = ALPHABET.length\n\nexport function generateShortcode(length = SHORTCODE_LENGTH): string {\n  // randomBytes is uniform; we discard bytes whose value falls in the bias\n  // window (>= 252 = floor(256/36)*36). Cheap, and avoids modulo bias.\n  const out: string[] = []\n  while (out.length < length) {\n    const buf = randomBytes(length * 2)\n    for (let i = 0; i < buf.length && out.length < length; i++) {\n      const b = buf[i]!\n      if (b >= 252) continue\n      out.push(ALPHABET[b % ALPHABET_LEN]!)\n    }\n  }\n  return out.join(\"\")\n}\n\nexport async function generateUniqueShortcode(\n  db: Transactable,\n  maxAttempts = 5,\n): Promise<string> {\n  for (let i = 0; i < maxAttempts; i++) {\n    const candidate = generateShortcode()\n    const [row] = await db\n      .select({ id: collections.id })\n      .from(collections)\n      .where(eq(collections.slug, candidate))\n      .limit(1)\n    if (!row) return candidate\n  }\n  throw new Error(\"could not generate a unique collection shortcode\")\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { and, eq, inArray, isNull, sql } from \"drizzle-orm\"\n\nimport { generateUniqueShortcode } from \"~~/server/utils/collections/shortcode\"\nimport { collectionItems, collections } from \"~~/shared/db/schema\"\nimport type {\n  CollectionVisibility,\n  CreateCollectionInput,\n  UpdateCollectionInput,\n} from \"~~/shared/validators/collection\"\n\nimport type { Transactable } from \"./types\"\n\n// `collections` + `collection_items` table accessor. The install\n// orchestrator uses these to make sure every install also drops a row into\n// the user's \"Installed\" system collection; the user-facing collections\n// feature reuses the same primitives.\n\nexport type SystemKind = \"installed\" | \"saved\"\n\nconst SYSTEM_KIND_LABEL: Record<SystemKind, string> = {\n  installed: \"Installed\",\n  saved: \"Saved\",\n}\n\nexport type CollectionErrorCode =\n  | \"not_found\"\n  | \"forbidden\"\n  | \"system_collection_locked\"\n\nexport class CollectionError extends Error {\n  readonly code: CollectionErrorCode\n  constructor(code: CollectionErrorCode) {\n    super(code)\n    this.code = code\n    this.name = \"CollectionError\"\n  }\n}\n\n// Look up (or create) the per-user system collection of the given kind.\n// Idempotent: safe to call concurrently — the worst case is two rows of\n// the same kind for the same user, which the install flow then both\n// `addItem`s into without breakage. If that race ever shows up in prod,\n// add a partial unique index on (owner_user_id, system_kind).\nexport async function getOrCreateSystem(\n  db: Transactable,\n  userId: string,\n  kind: SystemKind,\n): Promise<{ id: string; slug: string }> {\n  const existing = await db\n    .select({ id: collections.id, slug: collections.slug })\n    .from(collections)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId),\n        eq(collections.systemKind, kind),\n      ),\n    )\n    .limit(1)\n  if (existing[0]) return existing[0]\n\n  const id = crypto.randomUUID()\n  const slug = await generateUniqueShortcode(db)\n  await db.insert(collections).values({\n    id,\n    slug,\n    ownerUserId: userId,\n    name: SYSTEM_KIND_LABEL[kind],\n    systemKind: kind,\n  })\n  return { id, slug }\n}\n\nexport async function addItem(\n  db: Transactable,\n  collectionId: string,\n  extensionId: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  await db\n    .insert(collectionItems)\n    .values({ collectionId, extensionId })\n    .onConflictDoNothing()\n}\n\n// User-facing CRUD below — used by the /api/internal/collections routes.\n\nexport interface CollectionRow {\n  id: string\n  slug: string\n  ownerUserId: string\n  name: string\n  nameZh: string | null\n  description: string | null\n  descriptionZh: string | null\n  systemKind: SystemKind | null\n  visibility: CollectionVisibility\n  publishedAt: Date | null\n  createdAt: Date\n  updatedAt: Date\n}\n\nconst fullSelect = {\n  id: collections.id,\n  slug: collections.slug,\n  ownerUserId: collections.ownerUserId,\n  name: collections.name,\n  nameZh: collections.nameZh,\n  description: collections.description,\n  descriptionZh: collections.descriptionZh,\n  systemKind: collections.systemKind,\n  visibility: collections.visibility,\n  publishedAt: collections.publishedAt,\n  createdAt: collections.createdAt,\n  updatedAt: collections.updatedAt,\n}\n\nexport async function findById(\n  db: Transactable,\n  id: string,\n): Promise<CollectionRow | null> {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select(fullSelect)\n    .from(collections)\n    .where(eq(collections.id, id))\n    .limit(1)\n  return row ?? null\n}\n\nexport async function findBySlug(\n  db: Transactable,\n  slug: string,\n): Promise<CollectionRow | null> {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select(fullSelect)\n    .from(collections)\n    .where(eq(collections.slug, slug))\n    .limit(1)\n  return row ?? null\n}\n\nexport async function findByIdsForUser(\n  db: Transactable,\n  collectionIds: string[],\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<string[]> {\n  // Returns the subset that belongs to the given user — used by the\n  // for-extension membership query so we never leak another user's\n  // collection ids back to the client.\n  if (collectionIds.length === 0) return []\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({ id: collections.id })\n    .from(collections)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        inArray(collections.id, collectionIds),\n        eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId),\n      ),\n    )\n  return rows.map((r) => r.id)\n}\n\nexport async function create(\n  db: Transactable,\n  params: { ownerUserId: string; input: CreateCollectionInput },\n): Promise<CollectionRow> {\n  const { ownerUserId, input } = params\n  const id = crypto.randomUUID()\n  const slug = await generateUniqueShortcode(db)\n  const visibility = input.visibility ?? \"private\"\n  const now = new Date()\n  await db.insert(collections).values({\n    id,\n    slug,\n    ownerUserId,\n    name: input.name,\n    nameZh: input.nameZh ?? null,\n    description: input.description ?? null,\n    descriptionZh: input.descriptionZh ?? null,\n    visibility,\n    publishedAt: visibility === \"public\" ? now : null,\n    createdAt: now,\n    updatedAt: now,\n  })\n  const created = await findById(db, id)\n  if (!created) throw new Error(\"collection insert vanished\")\n  return created\n}\n\nexport async function update(\n  db: Transactable,\n  params: {\n    slug: string\n    ownerUserId: string\n    input: UpdateCollectionInput\n  },\n): Promise<CollectionRow> {\n  const existing = await findBySlug(db, params.slug)\n  if (!existing) throw new CollectionError(\"not_found\")\n  if (existing.ownerUserId !== params.ownerUserId) {\n    throw new CollectionError(\"forbidden\")\n  }\n  if (existing.systemKind !== null) {\n    throw new CollectionError(\"system_collection_locked\")\n  }\n\n  const patch: Partial<typeof collections.$inferInsert> = { updatedAt: new Date() }\n  const { input } = params\n  if (input.name !== undefined) patch.name = input.name\n  if (input.nameZh !== undefined) patch.nameZh = input.nameZh ?? null\n  if (input.description !== undefined) {\n    patch.description = input.description ?? null\n  }\n  if (input.descriptionZh !== undefined) {\n    patch.descriptionZh = input.descriptionZh ?? null\n  }\n  if (input.visibility !== undefined) {\n    patch.visibility = input.visibility\n    // Stamp publishedAt on the first private→public transition. Don't clear\n    // it on a flip back; \"ever-published\" sort order stays stable.\n    if (input.visibility === \"public\" && existing.publishedAt === null) {\n      patch.publishedAt = new Date()\n    }\n  }\n\n  await db.update(collections).set(patch).where(eq(collections.id, existing.id))\n  const after = await findById(db, existing.id)\n  if (!after) throw new Error(\"collection vanished mid-update\")\n  return after\n}\n\nexport async function remove(\n  db: Transactable,\n  params: { slug: string; ownerUserId: string },\n): Promise<void> {\n  const existing = await findBySlug(db, params.slug)\n  if (!existing) throw new CollectionError(\"not_found\")\n  if (existing.ownerUserId !== params.ownerUserId) {\n    throw new CollectionError(\"forbidden\")\n  }\n  if (existing.systemKind !== null) {\n    throw new CollectionError(\"system_collection_locked\")\n  }\n  await db.delete(collections).where(eq(collections.id, existing.id))\n}\n\nexport async function addItemBySlug(\n  db: Transactable,\n  params: { slug: string; ownerUserId: string; extensionId: string },\n): Promise<void> {\n  const existing = await findBySlug(db, params.slug)\n  if (!existing) throw new CollectionError(\"not_found\")\n  if (existing.ownerUserId !== params.ownerUserId) {\n    throw new CollectionError(\"forbidden\")\n  }\n  await db\n    .insert(collectionItems)\n    .values({ collectionId: existing.id, extensionId: params.extensionId })\n    .onConflictDoNothing()\n  await db\n    .update(collections)\n    .set({ updatedAt: new Date() })\n    .where(eq(collections.id, existing.id))\n}\n\nexport async function removeItemBySlug(\n  db: Transactable,\n  params: { slug: string; ownerUserId: string; extensionId: string },\n): Promise<void> {\n  const existing = await findBySlug(db, params.slug)\n  if (!existing) throw new CollectionError(\"not_found\")\n  if (existing.ownerUserId !== params.ownerUserId) {\n    throw new CollectionError(\"forbidden\")\n  }\n  await db\n    .delete(collectionItems)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(collectionItems.collectionId, existing.id),\n        eq(collectionItems.extensionId, params.extensionId),\n      ),\n    )\n  await db\n    .update(collections)\n    .set({ updatedAt: new Date() })\n    .where(eq(collections.id, existing.id))\n}\n\n// Membership lookup for the save-to-collection popover — returns the ids of\n// the user's collections that already contain the given extension.\nexport async function membershipForExtension(\n  db: Transactable,\n  params: { userId: string; extensionId: string },\n): Promise<string[]> {\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({ id: collections.id })\n    .from(collectionItems)\n    .innerJoin(collections, eq(collections.id, collectionItems.collectionId))\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(collections.ownerUserId, params.userId),\n        eq(collectionItems.extensionId, params.extensionId),\n      ),\n    )\n  return rows.map((r) => r.id)\n}\n\n// Filter helper used by callers that want to exclude system kinds in queries.\nexport function systemKindIsNull() {\n  return isNull(collections.systemKind)\n}\n\n// Item count subquery factory — kept here so the queries layer doesn't have\n// to know schema details.\nexport function itemCountExpression() {\n  return sql<number>`(SELECT count(*)::int FROM ${collectionItems} WHERE ${collectionItems.collectionId} = ${collections.id})`\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { and, desc, eq, ne, sql } from \"drizzle-orm\"\n\nimport { useDb } from \"~~/server/utils/db\"\nimport { users } from \"~~/shared/db/schema/auth\"\nimport { collectionItems, collections } from \"~~/shared/db/schema/collection\"\nimport { extensions } from \"~~/shared/db/schema/extension\"\n\nimport { itemCountExpression } from \"~~/server/repositories/collections\"\nimport type { CollectionVisibility } from \"~~/shared/validators/collection\"\n\n// View-layer aggregations for collections. CRUD lives in\n// `server/repositories/collections.ts`; this module is for the shapes that\n// the profile, browse, and detail pages need.\n\nexport interface OwnerCollectionRow {\n  id: string\n  slug: string\n  name: string\n  nameZh: string | null\n  description: string | null\n  descriptionZh: string | null\n  systemKind: \"installed\" | \"saved\" | null\n  visibility: CollectionVisibility\n  itemCount: number\n  updatedAt: Date\n}\n\nexport interface PublicCollectionRow {\n  slug: string\n  name: string\n  nameZh: string | null\n  description: string | null\n  descriptionZh: string | null\n  itemCount: number\n  publishedAt: Date | null\n  ownerName: string\n}\n\nexport interface CollectionItemRow {\n  extensionId: string\n  slug: string\n  name: string\n  nameZh: string | null\n  category: string\n  iconColor: string | null\n  iconEmoji: string | null\n  description: string | null\n  descriptionZh: string | null\n  addedAt: Date\n}\n\n// Owner-side listing. Excludes the 'installed' system row (that surface is\n// the dedicated \"Installed\" tab) but keeps 'saved' so the picker can pin it\n// at the top. The 'installed' row is still managed by the install\n// orchestrator — it's just hidden from the collections UI.\nexport async function listForOwner(\n  userId: string,\n): Promise<OwnerCollectionRow[]> {\n  const db = useDb()\n  return db\n    .select({\n      id: collections.id,\n      slug: collections.slug,\n      name: collections.name,\n      nameZh: collections.nameZh,\n      description: collections.description,\n      descriptionZh: collections.descriptionZh,\n      systemKind: collections.systemKind,\n      visibility: collections.visibility,\n      itemCount: itemCountExpression(),\n      updatedAt: collections.updatedAt,\n    })\n    .from(collections)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId),\n        // ne(systemKind, \"installed\") would also match the NULL systemKind\n        // rows (user-created), which is what we want.\n        sql`(${collections.systemKind} IS NULL OR ${collections.systemKind} <> 'installed')`,\n      ),\n    )\n    .orderBy(\n      // Pin 'saved' at the top, then user-created collections by most-recent.\n      sql`CASE WHEN ${collections.systemKind} = 'saved' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END`,\n      desc(collections.updatedAt),\n    )\n}\n\nexport async function listPublicPaged(params: {\n  page: number\n  pageSize: number\n}): Promise<{ rows: PublicCollectionRow[]; total: number }> {\n  const db = useDb()\n  const { page, pageSize } = params\n  const offset = Math.max(0, (page - 1) * pageSize)\n\n  const [rows, totalRow] = await Promise.all([\n    db\n      .select({\n        slug: collections.slug,\n        name: collections.name,\n        nameZh: collections.nameZh,\n        description: collections.description,\n        descriptionZh: collections.descriptionZh,\n        itemCount: itemCountExpression(),\n        publishedAt: collections.publishedAt,\n        ownerName: users.name,\n      })\n      .from(collections)\n      .innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, collections.ownerUserId))\n      .where(eq(collections.visibility, \"public\"))\n      .orderBy(desc(collections.publishedAt), desc(collections.id))\n      .limit(pageSize)\n      .offset(offset),\n    db\n      .select({ c: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })\n      .from(collections)\n      .where(eq(collections.visibility, \"public\")),\n  ])\n  return { rows, total: totalRow[0]?.c ?? 0 }\n}\n\nexport async function listItems(\n  collectionId: string,\n): Promise<CollectionItemRow[]> {\n  const db = useDb()\n  return db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,\n      description: extensions.description,\n      descriptionZh: extensions.descriptionZh,\n      addedAt: collectionItems.addedAt,\n    })\n    .from(collectionItems)\n    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, collectionItems.extensionId))\n    .where(eq(collectionItems.collectionId, collectionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(collectionItems.addedAt))\n}\n\n// Helper used by the [slug] detail route to also surface the owner's display\n// name for the \"by X\" line.\nexport async function getOwnerName(userId: string): Promise<string | null> {\n  const db = useDb()\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({ name: users.name })\n    .from(users)\n    .where(eq(users.id, userId))\n    .limit(1)\n  return row?.name ?? null\n}\n\n// Silence unused-import warning while still keeping the helper available.\nvoid ne\n"
}

4	export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
5	  const query = getQuery(event)
6	  const slug = typeof query.slug === "string" ? query.slug : null
7	  if (!slug) throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: "slug required" })
8	
9	  const db = useDb()

11	  if (!ext) throw createError({ statusCode: 404, statusMessage: "not_found" })
12	
13	  const [versions, related] = await Promise.all([
14	    versionsRepo.listForExtension(db, ext.id),
15	    extensionsRepo.findRelated(db, ext.id, ext.category),
16	  ])
17	
18	  return { ext, versions, related }
19	})

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/collections/[slug].get.ts",
  "content": "import * as collectionsRepo from \"~~/server/repositories/collections\"\nimport { getOwnerName, listItems } from \"~~/server/utils/queries/collections\"\n\n// Single collection. Public rows are readable by anyone (including signed-out\n// visitors); private rows only by the owner.\nexport default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  const slug = getRouterParam(event, \"slug\")\n  if (!slug) throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: \"slug_required\" })\n\n  const db = useDb()\n  const collection = await collectionsRepo.findBySlug(db, slug)\n  if (!collection) throw createError({ statusCode: 404, statusMessage: \"not_found\" })\n\n  const viewer = await getSessionUser(event)\n  const isOwner = viewer?.id === collection.ownerUserId\n  if (collection.visibility === \"private\" && !isOwner) {\n    throw createError({ statusCode: 404, statusMessage: \"not_found\" })\n  }\n\n  const [items, ownerName] = await Promise.all([\n    listItems(collection.id),\n    getOwnerName(collection.ownerUserId),\n  ])\n\n  return {\n    collection: {\n      id: collection.id,\n      slug: collection.slug,\n      name: collection.name,\n      nameZh: collection.nameZh,\n      description: collection.description,\n      descriptionZh: collection.descriptionZh,\n      visibility: collection.visibility,\n      systemKind: collection.systemKind,\n      ownerName,\n      isOwner,\n      itemCount: items.length,\n      publishedAt: collection.publishedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,\n      createdAt: collection.createdAt.toISOString(),\n      updatedAt: collection.updatedAt.toISOString(),\n    },\n    items: items.map((i) => ({ ...i, addedAt: i.addedAt.toISOString() })),\n  }\n})\n"
}

  "content": "import { listPublicPaged } from \"~~/server/utils/queries/collections\"\n\nconst DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 24\nconst MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 60\n\nexport default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  const query = getQuery(event)\n  const page = Math.max(1, Number.parseInt(String(query.page ?? \"1\"), 10) || 1)\n  const pageSize = Math.min(\n    MAX_PAGE_SIZE,\n    Math.max(\n      1,\n      Number.parseInt(String(query.pageSize ?? DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE), 10)\n        || DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE,\n    ),\n  )\n\n  const { rows, total } = await listPublicPaged({ page, pageSize })\n  return {\n    items: rows.map((r) => ({\n      ...r,\n      publishedAt: r.publishedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,\n    })),\n    total,\n    page,\n    pageSize,\n  }\n})\n"
}

  "content": "import * as collectionsRepo from \"~~/server/repositories/collections\"\n\n// Returns the ids of the signed-in user's collections that already contain\n// the given extension. Powers the save-to-collection popover's checkbox\n// state without a separate per-collection round-trip.\nexport default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  const user = await requireUser(event)\n  const query = getQuery(event)\n  const extensionId = typeof query.extensionId === \"string\" ? query.extensionId : null\n  if (!extensionId) {\n    throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: \"extensionId_required\" })\n  }\n\n  const ids = await collectionsRepo.membershipForExtension(useDb(), {\n    userId: user.id,\n    extensionId,\n  })\n  return { collectionIds: ids }\n})\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/utils/collections/errors.ts",
  "content": "import { CollectionError } from \"~~/server/repositories/collections\"\n\n// Single place that maps the repo's `CollectionError` codes onto HTTP\n// statuses. Routes call it from a `catch` so the mapping stays consistent\n// across the dozen endpoints.\nexport function mapCollectionError(err: unknown): ReturnType<typeof createError> {\n  if (err instanceof CollectionError) {\n    const status =\n      err.code === \"not_found\"\n        ? 404\n        : err.code === \"forbidden\"\n          ? 403\n          : err.code === \"system_collection_locked\"\n            ? 422\n            : 500\n    return createError({ statusCode: status, statusMessage: err.code })\n  }\n  console.error(\"unexpected collection error\", err)\n  return createError({ statusCode: 500, statusMessage: \"internal_error\" })\n}\n"
}
```

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import type { HTMLAttributes, InputHTMLAttributes } from "vue"
3	import { cn } from "~/lib/utils"
4	
5	const props = defineProps<{
6	  modelValue?: string | number | null
7	  type?: InputHTMLAttributes["type"]
8	  class?: HTMLAttributes["class"]
9	}>()
10	
11	const emit = defineEmits<{
12	  (e: "update:modelValue", value: string | number | null): void
13	}>()
14	
15	function onInput(event: Event) {
16	  const el = event.target as HTMLInputElement
17	  if (props.type === "number") {
18	    emit("update:modelValue", el.value === "" ? null : Number(el.value))
19	    return
20	  }
21	  emit("update:modelValue", el.value)
22	}
23	</script>
24	
25	<template>
26	  <input
27	    :type="props.type ?? 'text'"
28	    :value="modelValue ?? ''"
29	    :class="cn(

31	      'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)/40 focus-visible:border-(--color-accent)/60',
32	      'disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50',
33	      props.class,
34	    )"
35	    @input="onInput"
36	  >

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { Bookmark, BookmarkCheck, Loader2, Plus } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\n\nimport Checkbox from \"~/components/ui/Checkbox.vue\"\nimport Input from \"~/components/ui/Input.vue\"\nimport Popover from \"~/components/ui/Popover.vue\"\nimport PopoverContent from \"~/components/ui/PopoverContent.vue\"\nimport PopoverTrigger from \"~/components/ui/PopoverTrigger.vue\"\n\ninterface CollectionRow {\n  id: string\n  slug: string\n  name: string\n  nameZh: string | null\n  systemKind: \"installed\" | \"saved\" | null\n  visibility: \"private\" | \"public\"\n  itemCount: number\n}\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  extensionId: string\n  variant?: \"pill\" | \"icon\"\n}>()\n\nconst { t, locale } = useI18n()\nconst router = useRouter()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\nconst open = ref(false)\nconst loaded = ref(false)\nconst loading = ref(false)\nconst collections = ref<CollectionRow[]>([])\n// Set of collection ids the current extension belongs to. Mutated optimistically.\nconst memberIds = ref(new Set<string>())\n// Per-collection inflight flag, keyed by slug.\nconst pendingSlugs = ref(new Set<string>())\nconst newName = ref(\"\")\nconst creating = ref(false)\n\nconst isMember = computed(() => memberIds.value.size > 0)\n\nfunction displayName(c: CollectionRow): string {\n  if (c.systemKind === \"saved\") return t(\"collections.savedSystemName\")\n  if (locale.value === \"zh\" && c.nameZh) return c.nameZh\n  return c.name\n}\n\nasync function loadOnce() {\n  if (loaded.value || loading.value) return\n  loading.value = true\n  try {\n    const [list, membership] = await Promise.all([\n      $fetch<{ rows: CollectionRow[] }>(\"/api/internal/collections\"),\n      $fetch<{ collectionIds: string[] }>(\n Set(membership.collectionIds)\n    loaded.value = true\n  } catch (err) {\n    const statusCode = (err as { statusCode?: number })?.statusCode\n    if (statusCode === 401) {\n      open.value = false\n      await router.push(localePath(\"/sign-in\"))\n      return\n    }\n    console.error(\"[collections-popover] load failed\", err)\n  } finally {\n    loading.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nwatch(open, (v) => {\n  if (v) void loadOnce()\n})\n\nasync function toggle(c: CollectionRow) {\n  if (pendingSlugs.value.has(c.slug)) return\n  const wasMember = memberIds.value.has(c.id)\n  // Optimistic update.\n  const next = new Set(memberIds.value)\n  if (wasMember) next.delete(c.id)\n  else next.add(c.id)\n  memberIds.value = next\n  pendingSlugs.value.add(c.slug)\n\n  try {\n    if (wasMember) {\n      await $fetch(\n        `/api/internal/collections/${encodeURIComponent(c.slug)}/items/${encodeURIComponent(props.extensionId)}`,\n        { method: \"DELETE\" },\n      )\n      // Bump item count down.\n      c.itemCount = Math.max(0, c.itemCount - 1)\n    } else {\n      await $fetch(\n        `/api/internal/collections/${encodeURIComponent(c.slug)}/items`,\n        { method: \"POST\", body: { extensionId: props.extensionId } },\n      )\n      c.itemCount += 1\n    }\n  } catch (err) {\n    // Revert on failure.\n    const reverted = new Set(memberIds.value)\n    if (wasMember) reverted.add(c.id)\n    else reverted.delete(c.id)\n    memberIds.value = reverted\n    console.error(\"[collections-popover] toggle failed\", err)\n  } finally {\n    pendingSlugs.value.delete(c.slug)\n  }\n}\n\nasync function createAndAdd() {\n  const name = newName.value.trim()\n  if (!name || creating.value) return\n  creating.value = true\n  try {\n    const created = await $fetch<{ slug: string; id: string }>(\n      \"/api/internal/collections\",\n      { method: \"POST\", body: { name } },\n    )\n    // Immediately add the current extension to the new collection.\n    await $fetch(\n      `/api/internal/collections/${encodeURIComponent(created.slug)}/items`,\n      { method: \"POST\", body: { extensionId: props.extensionId } },\n    )\n    const row: CollectionRow = {\n      id: created.id,\n      slug: created.slug,\n      name,\n      nameZh: null,\n      systemKind: null,\n      visibility: \"private\",\n      itemCount: 1,\n    }\n    collections.value = [...collections.value, row]\n    const next = new Set(memberIds.value)\n    next.add(created.id)\n    memberIds.value = next\n    newName.value = \"\"\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[collections-popover] create failed\", err)\n  } finally {\n    creating.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nfunction onSubmitCreate(event: Event) {\n  event.preventDefault()\n  void createAndAdd()\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <Popover v-model:open=\"open\">\n    <PopoverTrigger as-child>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        :aria-label=\"isMember ? t('common.saved') BookmarkCheck : Bookmark\" :size=\"14\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n        <span v-if=\"variant !== 'icon'\">\n          {{ isMember ? t(\"common.saved\") : t(\"common.save\") }}\n        </span>\n      </button>\n    </PopoverTrigger>\n\n    <PopoverContent align=\"end\" class=\"w-80 p-0\">\n      <div class=\"border-b border-(--color-border) px-4 py-2.5\">\n        <h3 class=\"text-sm font-semibold  <label :for=\"`col-${c.id}`\" class=\"flex-1 min-w-0 cursor-pointer\">\n            <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n              <span class=\"truncate text-sm text-(--color-ink)\">{{ displayName(c) }}</span>\n              <span class=\"font-mono text-[10px] text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n                {{ c.itemCount }}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            <div v-if=\"c.systemKind === 'saved'\" class=\"text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n              {{ t(\"collections.popover.systemLabel\") }}\n            </div>\n            <div v-else-if=\"c.visibility === 'public'\" class=\"text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n              {{ t(\"collections.detail.visibilityPublic\") }}\n            </div>\n          </label>\n        </li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <form\n        class=\"flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-(--color-border) px-4 py-3\"\n 1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import {
3	  ChevronDown,
4	  Command,
5	  Globe2,
6	  Map,
7	  Menu,
8	  Plug,
9	  Search,
10	  Zap,
11	} from "lucide-vue-next"
12	import type { Component } from "vue"
13	
14	withDefaults(defineProps<{ collapsed: boolean; noSidebar?: boolean }>(), {
15	  noSidebar: false,
16	})
17	const emit = defineEmits<{ "toggle-sidebar": [] }>()
18	
19	const { t } = useI18n()
20	const route = useRoute()
21	const router = useRouter()
22	const localePath = useLocalePath()
23	
24	const q = ref<string>(typeof route.query.q === "string" ? route.query.q : "")
25	
26	watch(
27	  () => route.query.q,
28	  (next) => {
29	    q.value = typeof next === "string" ? next : ""
30	  },
31	)
32	
33	function onSubmit() {
34	  const query: Record<string, string> = {}
35	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
36	    if (typeof v === "string" && k !== "q" && k !== "page") query[k] = v
37	  }
38	  const trimmed = q.value.trim()
39	  if (trimmed) query.q = trimmed
40	  router.push({ path: localePath("/extensions"), query })
41	}
42	
43	type ExploreKey = "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
44	
45	const EXPLORE_ITEMS: { key: ExploreKey; labelKey: string; Icon: Component }[] = [
46	  { key: "skills", labelKey: "sidebar.skills", Icon: Zap },
47	  { key: "mcp", labelKey: "sidebar.mcpServers", Icon: Globe2 },
48	  { key: "slash", labelKey: "sidebar.slashCommands", Icon: Command },
49	  { key: "plugins", labelKey: "sidebar.plugins", Icon: Plug },
50	]
51	
52	const PANORAMA_ITEMS: { key: string; to: string; labelKey: string; Icon: Component }[] = [
53	  { key: "mcp-panorama", to: "/mcp-panorama", labelKey: "nav.mcpPanorama", Icon: Map },
54	]
55	
56	const exploreOpen = ref(false)
57	
58	// Explore is "active" whenever the user is on the extensions index or
59	// any of the panorama routes that live under the dropdown. The dropdown
60	// itself is type-first navigation — users must pick a category to enter
61	// /extensions — but `/extensions` (no filter) is still reachable as a
62	// fallback, so the trigger should light up there too.
63	const localeExtensionsPath = computed(() => localePath("/extensions"))
64	const localePanoramaPaths = computed(() =>
65	  PANORAMA_ITEMS.map((p) => localePath(p.to)),
66	)
67	const exploreActive = computed(() => {
68	  if (route.path === localeExtensionsPath.value) return true
69	  if (route.path.startsWith(`${localeExtensionsPath.value}/`)) return true
70	  return localePanoramaPaths.value.some(
71	    (p) => route.path === p || route.path.startsWith(`${p}/`),
72	  )
73	})
74	</script>
75	
76	<template>
77	  <div class="contents">
78	    <button
79	      v-if="!noSidebar"
80	      type="button"
81	      class="p-2 rounded hover:bg-(--color-sidebar) focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)"
82	      :aria-label="t('nav.toggleSidebar')"
83	      :aria-expanded="!collapsed"
84	      @click="emit('toggle-sidebar')"
85	    >
86	      <Menu :size="20" aria-hidden="true" />
87	    </button>
88	
89	    <NuxtLink
90	      :to="localePath('/')"
91	      class="font-serif text-lg tracking-tight hover:opacity-80"
92	    >
93	      AgentCenter
94	    </NuxtLink>
95	
96	    <form
97	      role="search"
98	      class="flex-1 max-w-xl mx-auto relative"
99	      @submit.prevent="onSubmit"
100	    >
101	      <label class="sr-only" for="topbar-search">{{ t("nav.searchLabel") }}</label>
102	      <Search
103	        :size="16"
104	        class="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-(--color-ink-muted) pointer-events-none"
105	        aria-hidden="true"
106	      />
107	      <input
108	        id="topbar-search"
109	        v-model="q"
110	        type="search"
111	        :placeholder="t('search.placeholder')"
112	        class="w-full h-9 pl-9 pr-3 rounded-md border border-transparent bg-(--color-sidebar)/60 text-sm transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:border-(--color-border) focus:bg-(--color-bg) focus:ring-2 focus:ring-(--color-accent)"
113	      >
114	    </form>
115	
116	    <nav class="hidden md:flex items-center gap-1 text-sm" :aria-label="t('nav.explore')">
117	      <Popover v-model:open="exploreOpen">
118	        <PopoverTrigger
119	          class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)"
120	          :class="exploreActive ? 'text-(--color-ink) font-semibold bg-(--color-sidebar)/60' : ''"
121	        >
122	          {{ t("nav.explore") }}
123	          <ChevronDown :size="12" aria-hidden="true" />
124	        </PopoverTrigger>

126	          <NuxtLink

128	            :key="item.key"
129	            :to="{ path: localeExtensionsPath, query: { category: item.key } }"
130	            class="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-2 text-[14px] text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-card)"

<script setup lang="ts">
import type { ProfileSectionKey } from "~/components/profile/SectionRail.vue"

definePageMeta({ middleware: ["require-auth", "require-onboard"] })

const { t } = useI18n()
const route = useRoute()

const SECTIONS: ReadonlyArray<ProfileSectionKey> = [
  "installed",
  "published",
  "drafts",
  "saved",
  "activity",
  "settings",
]

const active = computed<ProfileSectionKey>(() => {
  const raw = String(route.query.section ?? "installed")
  return (SECTIONS as ReadonlyArray<string>).includes(raw)
    ? (raw as ProfileSectionKey)
    : "installed"
})

// Response types are inferred from server/api/internal/profile/{me,section}.get.ts
// via Nuxt's auto-generated InternalApi map (see .nuxt/types/nitro-routes.d.ts).
// No manual duplication needed.
const { data: me, error: meError, refresh: refreshMe } = await useFetch(
  "/api/internal/profile/me",
)

const sectionQuery = computed(() =>
  active.value === "settings" ? null : { section: active.value },
)

const { data: section, error: sectionError } = await useFetch(
  "/api/internal/profile/section",
  {
    query: sectionQuery,
    // Skip the fetch when on the Settings tab.
    immediate: active.value !== "settings",
  },
)
</script>

<template>
  <div class="px-6 py-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
    <div
      v-if="meError"
      class="rounded-(--radius-card) border border-red-300/40 bg-red-50/40 p-6 text-sm text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/10"
```

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { FolderOpen } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\n\nimport CollectionCard from \"~/components/collection/CollectionCard.vue\"\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\nconst page = computed(() => {\n  const raw = Number.parseInt(String(route.query.page ?? \"1\"), 10)\n  return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw > 0 ? raw : 1\n})\n\nconst { data, pending, error } = await useFetch(\n  \"/api/internal/collections/public\",\n  { query: computed(() => ({ page: page.value })) },\n)\n\nconst totalPages = computed(() => {\n  if (!data.value) return 1\n  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.value.total / data.value.pageSize))\n})\n\nfunction pageLink(n: number) {\n  return { path: localePath(\"/collections\"), query: { page: n } }\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"px-6 py-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto\">\n    <header class=\"mb-8\">\n      <h1 class=\"font-serif text-3xl text-(--color-ink)\">\n        {{ t(\"collections.browse.title\") }}\n      </h1>\n      <p class=\"mt-2 text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n        {{ t(\"collections.browse.subtitle\") }}\n      </p>\n    </header>\n\n    <p v-if=\"error\" class=\"text-sm text-red-600\">\n      {{ error.statusMessage ?? error.message }}\n    </p>\n\n    <div v-else-if=\"pending\" class=\"grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3\">\n      <div\n        v-for=\"i in 6\"\n        :key=\"i\"\n        class=\"h-32 animate-pulse rounded-(--radius-card) border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card)/40\"\n      />\n    </div>\n\n    <div\n      v-else-if=\"!data || data.items.length === 0\"\n      class=\"rounded-(--radius-card) border border-dashed border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card)/40 p-10 text-center\"\n    >\n      <FolderOpen class=\"mx-auto size-8 text-(--color-ink-muted)\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n      <h3 class=\"mt-3 font-serif text-lg text-(--color-ink)\">\n        {{ t(\"collections.browse.empty\") }}\n      </h3>\n    </div>\n\n    <template v-else>\n      <div class=\"grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3\">\n        <CollectionCard\n          v-for=\"item in data.items\"\n          :key=\"item.slug\"\n          :slug=\"item.slug\"\n          :name=\"item.name\"\n          :name-zh=\"item.nameZh\"\n          :description=\"item.description\"\n          :description-zh=\"item.descriptionZh\"\n          :item-count=\"item.itemCount\"\n          :owner-name=\"item.ownerName\"\n          :show-owner=\"true\"\n          visibility=\"public\"\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      <nav\n        v-if=\"totalPages > 1\"\n        class=\"mt-8 flex items-center justify-center gap-2\"\n        :aria-label=\"t('collections.browse.pagination')\"\n      >\n        <NuxtLink\n          v-for=\"n in totalPages\"\n          :key=\"n\"\n          :to=\"pageLink(n)\"\n          class=\"rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-sm transition-colors\"\n          :class=\"n === page\n            ? 'border-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent) font-medium'\n            : 'border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'\"\n        >\n          {{ n }}\n        </NuxtLink>\n      </nav>\n    </template>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

\"\"\n})\n\nconst shareUrl = computed(() => {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") return \"\"\n  return `${window.location.origin}${localePath(`/collections/${slug.value}`)}`\n})\n\nconst isPublic = computed(() => data.value?.collection.visibility === \"public\")\nconst canEdit = computed(() => {\n  if (!data.value?.collection.isOwner) return false\n  return data.value.collection.systemKind === null\n})\n\nasync function copyLink() {\n  if (!shareUrl.value) return\n  try {\n    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl.value)\n    copied.value = true\n    setTimeout(() => { copied.value = false }, 2000)\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[collection] copy failed\", err)\n  }\n}\n\nasync function onEditSubmit(payload: {\n  name: string\n  nameZh?: string\n  description?: string\n  descriptionZh?: string\n  visibility: \"private\" | \"public\"\n}) {\n  busy.value = true\n  try {\n    await $fetch(`/api/internal/collections/${encodeURIComponent(slug.value)}`, {\n      method: \"PATCH\",\n      body: payload,\n    })\n    editing.value = false\n    await refresh()\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[collection] update failed\", err)\n  } finally {\n    busy.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function onToggleVisibility() {\n  if (!data.value || busy.value) return\n  busy.value = true\n  const next = data.value.collection.visibility === \"public\" ? error.message }}</p>\n    </div>\n\n    <template v-else-if=\"data\">\n      <header class=\"mb-6\">\n        <div class=\"flex items-start gap-3\">\n          <component\n            :is=\"data.collection.systemKind === 'saved' ? Globe : Lock\"\n              :size=\"12\"\n              aria-hidden=\"true\"\n            />\n            {{\n              isPublic\n                ? t(\"collections.detail.visibilityPublic\")\n                : t(\"collections.detail.visibilityPrivate\")\n            }}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div\n          v-if=\"data.collection.isOwner\"\n          class=\"mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\"\n        >\n          <button\n            v-if=\"isPublic\"\n            type=\"button\"\n            class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)\"\n            @click=\"copyLink\"\n          >\n            <component :is=\"copied ?     </h2>\n\n        <div\n          v-if=\"data.items.length === 0\"\n          class=\"rounded-(--radius-card) border border-dashed border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card)/40 p-10 text-center\"\n        >\n          <p class=\"text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)\">{{ t(\"collections.detail.empty\") }}</p>\n        </div>\n\n        <ul v-else class=\"grid gap-2\">\n          <li\n > AGENT

Lint, typecheck, and 242 tests all green. Summary of what landed:

**Schema (`shared/db/schema/collection.ts` + `drizzle/0007_wakeful_cloak.sql`)**
- New columns on `collections`: `slug` (10-char base36 shortcode, unique), `description`/`descriptionZh`, `visibility` (`private`/`public` enum), `publishedAt`, `updatedAt`.
- Indexes on `ownerUserId` and `(visibility, publishedAt DESC)`.
- Hand-edited migration adds `slug` nullable → backfills via `md5(id||rand())` → flips to NOT NULL + UNIQUE so the upgrade is safe on existing rows.

**Server**
- `server/utils/collections/shortcode.ts` — uniform base36 generator + DB-checked unique wrapper.
- `server/utils/collections/errors.ts` — `mapCollectionError` HTTP mapping.
- `server/repositories/collections.ts` — extended with `findBySlug`, `create`, `update` (handles first private→public publishedAt stamp), `remove`, `addItemBySlug`, `removeItemBySlug`, `membershipForExtension`, `CollectionError`. System collections rejected from edit/delete with `system_collection_locked`.
- `server/utils/queries/collections.ts` — `listForOwner` (pins `saved` at top, hides `installed`), `listPublicPaged`, `listItems`, `getOwnerName`.
- `server/utils/installs.ts` test stub updated to match new `{id, slug}` return.

**API routes (POST-based mutations to match codebase convention)**
- `GET/POST /api/internal/collections`
- `GET /api/internal/collections/[slug]` (ACL: public visible to anyone, private to owner only)
- `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/update`
- `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/delete`
- `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/items`
- `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/items/[extensionId]/remove`
- `GET /api/internal/collections/public` (paginated browse)
- `GET /api/internal/collection-memberships?extensionId=…` (named outside `[slug]` to dodge InternalApi type collisions)

**Validators** — `shared/validators/collection.ts` (Create / Update / AddItem zod schemas with trimming + empty-string→undefined coercion).

**UI**
- `SaveToCollectionPopover.vue` — checkbox list + inline "New collection" creator; "Saved" pinned at top; optimistic toggles with rollback. `SaveButton.vue` becomes a thin wrapper.
- `app/pages/collections/index.vue` — public browse grid with pagination.
- `app/pages/collections/[slug].vue` — detail page with owner-only edit/visibility/delete bar, copy-link affordance once public, item remove.
- `app/components/collection/{CollectionCard,CollectionForm}.vue` — shared card + form.
- `app/components/profile/SectionCollections.vue` + `SectionRail.vue` extended; "Collections" section added to profile.
- TopBar gets a `Collections` nav link between Explore and Publish.

**i18n** — `nav.collections`, `profile.sections.collections`, `profile.emptyCollections.*`, full `collections.*` namespace (browse, popover, form, actions, detail) in both `en.json` and `zh.json`.

The system `Saved` collection is preserved unchanged for `recordInstall`; new lazy-init goes through the same repository helper. Next step would be a smoke pass with `bun run dev` — and per the plan, the `/api/v1` public registry contract is untouched.

import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { resolve } from "node:path"

import { defineConfig } from "drizzle-kit"

const envPath = resolve(".env")
if (existsSync(envPath)) {
  const content = readFileSync(envPath, "utf-8")
  for (const line of content.split("\n")) {
    const match = line.match(/^([A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*)=(.*)$/)
    if (match && !process.env[match[1]]) {
      process.env[match[1]] = match[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "")
    }
  }
}

const databaseUrl = process.env.DATABASE_URL
if (!databaseUrl) {
  throw new Error(
    "Missing DATABASE_URL env var required for Drizzle Kit. " +
      "Set it in .env or your shell before running db:* scripts.",
  )
}

export default defineConfig({
  schema: "./shared/db/schema/index.ts",
  out: "./drizzle",
  dialect: "postgresql",
  dbCredentials: { url: databaseUrl },
  casing: "snake_case",
```

| Check | Result |

| `GET /api/internal/collections` lazy-seeds Saved | ✓ |
| Create public collection (bilingual name + description) | returns slug `olqomzsyw3`, visibility `public`, publishedAt stamped |
| Add extension `ext-1` to the collection | ✓, idempotent |
| Owner GET sees `itemCount: 1`, the item, `isOwner: true` | ✓ |
| Anonymous GET on public collection | 200 |
| Flip to private → anonymous GET | 404 (no info leak) |
| publishedAt stays populated through visibility toggle | ✓ (stable browse sort) |
| Membership lookup `?extensionId=ext-1` | returns the collection id |
| `DELETE` the Saved system collection | 422 `system_collection_locked` |

## 13. Commit & PR rules

The original Next.js project locked "Conventional Commits, one coherent unit per commit, phase scope." That carries over verbatim. This section spells out the parts that were left informal — granularity, breaking-change handling for `/api/v1`, merge strategy, the per-commit vs. per-PR validate split, and the hook tooling.

### Commit message format

```
<type>(<scope>): <one-line summary, lowercase, no trailing period>

<body — optional. Why, not what. Wraps at 72 chars.
Reference incidents, surprising decisions, or invariants the diff doesn't show.>

<footer — optional. BREAKING CHANGE, Closes #N, …>
```

### Types

`feat`, `fix`, `refactor`, `test`, `docs`, `chore`, `perf`, `revert`, `style`. Use the type that describes the dominant change in the commit. A commit that's "feat plus its tests" is `feat`, not split.

### Scopes

Two scope shapes used here:

- **Phase scope** during the v1 build: `p<N>-<short>` matching §10. Example: `feat(p3-browse): wire filter chips to query string`. Use this for any commit that lands a phase deliverable.
- **Feature scope** for hotfixes during v1 and all post-v1 work: a short noun for the area touched. Examples: `fix(filter-bar): preserve dept on locale switch`, `refactor(install-button): extract install state machine`, `chore(deps): bump drizzle 0.45 → 0.46`.

If a commit spans two scopes evenly, that's a smell — split it.

### Commit granularity — one coherent unit per commit

A phase is one or several commits. Default to **one coherent unit per commit** — a single focused change that compiles and stands on its own. The exact number of commits per phase depends on the work: a mechanical port may be one commit per phase; a feature build may be several. Optimise for "the diff tells a clear story", not for a target commit count.

Calibration:

| Granularity | Example | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| Too small | `feat: rename variable in ext-card.vue` | No — file-by-file noise |
| Right | `chore(p0): install @nuxt/eslint and base config` | Yes |
| Right | `feat(p2-db): add extension drizzle schema` | Yes |
| Right | `feat(p3-browse): ExtCard component + test` | Yes — code + its tests usually one commit |
| Right | `feat(p3-browse): wire ExtCard into home grid` | Yes |
| Right | `feat(p15-ui): port shadcn-vue primitives` covering ~28 generated wrappers (Button + Input + Label + Textarea + Checkbox + Skeleton + Dialog × 6 + Sheet × 4 + Popover × 3 + Select × 5 + Tabs × 4) | Yes — single mechanical unit |
| Too big | `feat(p3-browse): browse page` covering 8 unrelated files | No — split where the story changes |

Tests usually live with the code they cover in the same commit. The exception is adding tests to *existing* code as a coverage-improvement pass — then the test commit stands alone.

Always-split boundaries (these go in separate commits even if "small"):

- Database schema / migration vs. application code that uses it.
- Refactor vs. new feature (refactor first; feature on top).
- Generated code (migrations, lockfile updates) vs. source.
- Dependency install (`chore`) vs. first use (`feat`).

### Per-commit vs. per-PR validate

The validate suite has two gates with different strictness:

- **Each commit must compile and `bun run typecheck` must pass.** This is the per-step gate. Strict enough to keep history bisectable; loose enough that you can land a Zod validator in one commit before its consumer in the next.
- **The PR head must pass full `bun run validate`** (lint + typecheck + test + coverage thresholds). This is the per-PR gate. Enforced by the `pre-push` hook locally and by CI on the PR.
--

{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"feat(collections): user-curated collections with public sharing\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nTurns the existing implicit-bookmark schema (the `systemKind='saved'` collection) into a real \"save and organize\" surface: users create named collections (bilingual name + description), add/remove extensions, and toggle each collection between **private** and **public**. Public collections get a stable, copy-pasteable `/collections/<shortcode>` URL and appear on a `/collections` browse page.\n\n- The bookmark button on extension pages opens a checkbox-list popover (Saved pinned at top, user collections below, inline \"+ New collection\") instead of a single toggle.\n- Profile gains a \"Collections\" tab next to \"Saved\".\n- TopBar gains a \"Collections\" nav entry.\n- Web scope only — `/api/v1` is **untouched** so the CLI keeps working unchanged. CLI install-collection is deferred per the plan.\n\n## Schema\n\n`shared/db/schema/collection.ts` gains `slug` (10-char base36, unique), `description`/`descriptionZh`, `visibility` enum (`private` | `public`), `publishedAt` (stamped on first private→public flip), `updatedAt`, plus indexes on `ownerUserId` and `(visibility, publishedAt DESC)`.\n\nMigration `drizzle/0007_wakeful_cloak.sql` was hand-edited to add `slug` nullable → backfill via `md5(id||rand())` → flip to NOT NULL + UNIQUE so the upgrade is safe on existing rows.\n\n## API surface (internal)\n\n| Route | Purpose |\n| --- | --- |\n| `GET /api/internal/collections` | Owner list; lazy-seeds Saved |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections` | Create |\n| `GET /api/internal/collections/[slug]` | Public to anyone, private to owner only |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/update` | Rename / edit copy / visibility toggle |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/delete` | Owner-only; system collections return 422 |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/items` | Add extension (idempotent) |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/items/[extensionId]/remove` | Remove |\n| `GET /api/internal/collections/public` | Paginated browse |\n| `GET /api/internal/collection-memberships?extensionId=…` | Powers the popover checkbox state |\n\n`/api/v1` is **unchanged**.\n\n## Pivotal decisions\n\n1. **Two-state visibility** (`private` | `public`). Unlisted deferred — the enum can grow without a migration if needed.\n2. **`/collections/<shortcode>`** URLs, same path for public + private; visitors hitting a private one get a 404 (no info leak vs. truly missing).\n3. **POST-based mutations** instead of PATCH/DELETE on `[slug]` — matches the codebase's existing `publish/submit.post` convention and sidesteps a Nitro InternalApi type collision that broke `$fetch` method typing.\n4. **System \"Saved\" stays.** The picker pins it at the top; the existing recordInstall flow still writes to it via the shared `getOrCreateSystem` helper (now also returning the slug it generated).\n\n## Test plan\n\nEnd-to-end was already verified against a running dev server:\n\n- [x] `bun run db:migrate` against the existing local DB — clean, journal updated.\n- [x] Sign-up → list collections (lazy-seeded Saved row appears) → create a public collection → add `ext-1` → owner GET shows `itemCount:1`.\n- [x] Anonymous GET on the public collection returns 200; after `update {visibility:\"private\"}` returns 404.\n- [x] `publishedAt` stays populated across visibility toggles (stable browse sort).\n- [x] `POST .../[saved-slug]/delete` returns 422 with `system_collection_locked`.\n- [x] `/en/collections` and `/en/collections/[slug]` SSR-render correctly.\n- [x] `/zh/collections/[slug]` shows bilingual `nameZh` + Chinese visibility chip.\n- [x] `bun run validate` (lint + typecheck + 242/242 tests) green.\n\nStill to do post-merge:\n- [ ] Browser-level smoke test (popover click, \"+ New collection\", visibility toggle, copy-link affordance).\n- [ ] Add `server/utils/queries/collections.test.ts` and `shared/validators/collection.test.ts` integration coverage.\n- [ ] Decide whether to fold the existing \"Saved\" tab into the new \"Collections\" tab in a follow-up.\n\n## Out of scope (deferred)\n\n- No `/api/v1/collections/*` public registry endpoints. CLI cannot install-by-collection yet.\n- No org/department-scoped collections (only user-owned). `ownerOrgId` can be added later.\n- No featured/curated collections on the home page.\n- No fork, rating, or comments on collections.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Open PR with summary + test plan"
}
```

42	function flattenDepts(
43	  list: Department[],

60	}

102	## Tags

104	${ext.tags.map((t) => `\`${t}\``).join(" · ")}

203	  }[] = []
204	  for (const row of extRows) {
205	    const key = `${row.publisherUserId}|${row.ownerOrgId}`
206	    if (seen.has(key)) continue
207	    seen.add(key)
208	    membershipRows.push({
209	      id: `mem-${row.publisherUserId}-${row.ownerOrgId}`,
210	      userId: row.publisherUserId,
211	      orgId: row.ownerOrgId,
212	      role: "publisher",
213	    })
214	  }
215	  console.log(`seed: inserting ${membershipRows.length} memberships`)
216	  await db.insert(memberships).values(membershipRows)
217	
218	  const extTagRows = EXTENSIONS.flatMap((e) =>
219	    e.tags.map((tagKey) => ({
220	      extensionId: `ext-${e.id}`,
221	      tagId: tagKey,
222	    })),
223	  )
224	  console.log(`seed: inserting ${extTagRows.length} extension-tag links`)
225	  await db.insert(extensionTags).values(extTagRows)
226	
227	  console.log(
228	    `seed: skipping ${COLLECTIONS.length} mock collections (user-owned, seeded post-signup in P9)`,
229	  )
230	
231	  // ─── MCP Panorama landscape ────────────────────────────────────────────────
232	  // Shared with scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts (the standalone Vercel-build
233	  // seed). Both go through the same upsert path so the demo and prod stay
234	  // in sync. Uses ON CONFLICT so re-running is safe.
235	  await seedMcpLandscape(db)
236	
237	  console.log("seed: done")
238	  await client.end()
239	}
240	
241	main()
242	  .then(() => process.exit(0))
243	  .catch((err) => {
244	    console.error("seed: failed")
245	    console.error(err)
246	    process.exit(1)
247	  })
248	
```

> DEVELOPER

is it better to show the extension as same card as in extension list page? what do you think?

> AGENT

Yes — for the detail page, switching to `ExtCard` makes sense because a public collection is essentially a curated slice of the marketplace, so visitors deciding whether to install need the same signal (icon, downloads, stars, tags) the browse cards give them. The tradeoff is the owner-only "remove" control needs a tasteful spot — I'd put it as a hover overlay or behind an "Edit" mode toggle rather than glue a trash icon onto every card. I'd keep the profile **Saved** tab as the compact row though — it's a personal bookmarks view where scanability wins.

Want me to swap the items in `/collections/[slug].vue` over to `ExtCard`?

1	import { and, desc, eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
2	
3	import { itemCountExpression } from "~~/server/repositories/collections"
4	import { useDb } from "~~/server/utils/db"
5	import { users } from "~~/shared/db/schema/auth"
6	import { collectionItems, collections } from "~~/shared/db/schema/collection"
7	import { extensions } from "~~/shared/db/schema/extension"
8	import type { CollectionVisibility } from "~~/shared/validators/collection"
9	
10	// View-layer aggregations for collections. CRUD lives in
11	// `server/repositories/collections.ts`; this module is for the shapes that
12	// the profile, browse, and detail pages need.
13	
14	export interface OwnerCollectionRow {
15	  id: string
16	  slug: string
17	  name: string
18	  nameZh: string | null
19	  description: string | null
20	  descriptionZh: string | null
21	  systemKind: "installed" | "saved" | null
22	  visibility: CollectionVisibility
23	  itemCount: number
24	  updatedAt: Date
25	}
26	
27	export interface PublicCollectionRow {
28	  slug: string
29	  name: string
30	  nameZh: string | null
31	  description: string | null
32	  descriptionZh: string | null
33	  itemCount: number
34	  publishedAt: Date | null
35	  ownerName: string | null
36	}
37	
38	export interface CollectionItemRow {
39	  extensionId: string
40	  slug: string
41	  name: string
42	  nameZh: string | null
43	  category: string
44	  iconColor: string | null
45	  iconEmoji: string | null
46	  description: string | null
47	  descriptionZh: string | null
48	  addedAt: Date
49	}
50	
51	// Owner-side listing. Excludes the 'installed' system row (that surface is
52	// the dedicated "Installed" tab) but keeps 'saved' so the picker can pin it
53	// at the top. The 'installed' row is still managed by the install
54	// orchestrator — it's just hidden from the collections UI.
55	export async function listForOwner(
56	  userId: string,
57	): Promise<OwnerCollectionRow[]> {
58	  const db = useDb()
59	  return db
60	    .select({
61	      id: collections.id,
62	      slug: collections.slug,
63	      name: collections.name,
64	      nameZh: collections.nameZh,
65	      description: collections.description,
66	      descriptionZh: collections.descriptionZh,
67	      systemKind: collections.systemKind,
68	      visibility: collections.visibility,
69	      itemCount: itemCountExpression(),
70	      updatedAt: collections.updatedAt,
71	    })
72	    .from(collections)
73	    .where(
74	      and(
75	        eq(collections.ownerUserId, userId),
76	        // ne(systemKind, "installed") would also match the NULL systemKind
77	        // rows (user-created), which is what we want.
78	        sql`(${collections.systemKind} IS NULL OR ${collections.systemKind} <> 'installed')`,
79	      ),
80	    )
81	    .orderBy(
82	      // Pin 'saved' at the top, then user-created collections by most-recent.
83	      sql`CASE WHEN ${collections.systemKind} = 'saved' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END`,
84	      desc(collections.updatedAt),
85	    )
86	}
87	
88	export async function listPublicPaged(params: {
89	  page: number
90	  pageSize: number
91	}): Promise<{ rows: PublicCollectionRow[]; total: number }> {
92	  const db = useDb()
93	  const { page, pageSize } = params
94	  const offset = Math.max(0, (page - 1) * pageSize)
95	
96	  const [rows, totalRow] = await Promise.all([
97	    db
98	      .select({
99	        slug: collections.slug,
100	        name: collections.name,
101	        nameZh: collections.nameZh,
102	        description: collections.description,
103	        descriptionZh: collections.descriptionZh,
104	        itemCount: itemCountExpression(),
105	        publishedAt: collections.publishedAt,
106	        ownerName: users.name,
107	      })
108	      .from(collections)
109	      .innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, collections.ownerUserId))
110	      .where(eq(collections.visibility, "public"))
111	      .orderBy(desc(collections.publishedAt), desc(collections.id))
112	      .limit(pageSize)
113	      .offset(offset),
114	    db
115	      .select({ c: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
116	      .from(collections)
117	      .where(eq(collections.visibility, "public")),
118	  ])
119	  return { rows, total: totalRow[0]?.c ?? 0 }
120	}
121	
122	export async function listItems(
123	  collectionId: string,
124	): Promise<CollectionItemRow[]> {
125	  const db = useDb()
126	  return db
127	    .select({
128	      extensionId: extensions.id,
129	      slug: extensions.slug,
130	      name: extensions.name,
131	      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,
132	      category: extensions.category,
133	      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,
134	      iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,
135	      description: extensions.description,
136	      descriptionZh: extensions.descriptionZh,
137	      addedAt: collectionItems.addedAt,
138	    })
139	    .from(collectionItems)
140	    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, collectionItems.extensionId))
141	    .where(eq(collectionItems.collectionId, collectionId))
142	    .orderBy(desc(collectionItems.addedAt))
143	}
144	
145	// Helper used by the [slug] detail route to also surface the owner's display
146	// name for the "by X" line.
147	export async function getOwnerName(userId: string): Promise<string | null> {
148	  const db = useDb()
149	  const [row] = await db
150	    .select({ name: users.name })
151	    .from(users)
152	    .where(eq(users.id, userId))
153	    .limit(1)
154	  return row?.name ?? null
155	}
156	
```

  "old_string": "export async function listItems(\n  collectionId: string,\n): Promise<CollectionItemRow[]> {\n  const db = useDb()\n  return db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,\n      description: extensions.description,\n      descriptionZh: extensions.descriptionZh,\n      addedAt: collectionItems.addedAt,\n    })\n    .from(collectionItems)\n    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, collectionItems.extensionId))\n    .where(eq(collectionItems.collectionId, collectionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(collectionItems.addedAt))\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function listItems(\n  collectionId: string,\n): Promise<CollectionItemRow[]> {\n  // Mirrors the listSelect shape in server/repositories/extensions.ts so the\n  // detail page can pass each row straight into <ExtCard :ext=\"…\">. tagIds\n  // come from a leftJoin + array_agg + GROUP BY, exactly like the browse grid.\n  const db = useDb()\n  return db\n    .select({\n      id: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      badge: extensions.badge,\n      scope: extensions.scope,\n      funcCat: extensions.funcCat,\n      subCat: extensions.subCat,\n      l2: extensions.l2,\n      deptId: extensions.deptId,\n      iconEmoji: extensions.iconEmoji,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,\n      tagline: extensions.tagline,\n      taglineZh: extensions.taglineZh,\n      description: extensions.description,\n      descriptionZh: extensions.descriptionZh,\n      downloadsCount: extensions.downloadsCount,\n      starsAvg: extensions.starsAvg,\n      tagIds: sql<string[]>`coalesce(array_agg(${extensionTags.tagId}) FILTER (WHERE ${extensionTags.tagId} IS NOT NULL), '{}')`,\n      addedAt: collectionItems.addedAt,\n    })\n    .from(collectionItems)\n    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, collectionItems.extensionId))\n    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))\n    .where(eq(collectionItems.collectionId, collectionId))\n    .groupBy(extensions.id, collectionItems.addedAt)\n    .orderBy(desc(collectionItems.addedAt))\n}"
}
```

The thin row downplayed the items.\n\n`listItems` now mirrors the listSelect shape in extensions repo\n(badge, scope, deptId, downloadsCount, starsAvg, tagIds via array_agg),\nso each row drops straight into <ExtCard :ext=\"…\"> without an adapter.\n\nOwner-only remove sits as a small X badge in the card's top-right corner,\nz-10 above ExtCard's after:inset-0 click-overlay and stopping click\npropagation so it doesn't navigate to the extension page on press.\n\nProfile's \"Saved\" tab stays as the compact row — that's a personal\nbookmarks view where scanability wins; this card swap is just for the\nshared/detail surface.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",

<details>
<summary>📝 Walkthrough</summary>

## Walkthrough

This PR introduces a complete collections feature for organizing and sharing extensions. The implementation spans database schema updates, server-side CRUD operations and APIs, client-side UI components, collection detail/browse pages, profile section integration, and full i18n support for English and Chinese users.

## Changes

**Collections Feature**

| Layer / File(s) | Summary |
|---|---|
| **Database schema, migration, and shared types** <br> `drizzle/0007_wakeful_cloak.sql`, `drizzle/meta/0007_snapshot.json`, `drizzle/meta/_journal.json`, `shared/db/schema/collection.ts`, `shared/types.ts` | Migration adds `slug`, `visibility` enum, `publishedAt`, and `updatedAt` columns with backfill and indexes; snapshot captures complete schema state; ProfileCollectionRow type defined for client-server contracts. |
| **Shared validators and input constants** <br> `shared/validators/collection.ts` | Zod schemas for CreateCollectionInput, UpdateCollectionInput, AddItemInput; collection name/description sizing constants; CollectionVisibility enum with transformations for localized fields. |
| **Server repository, error mapping, and query utilities** <br> `server/repositories/collections.ts`, `server/utils/collections/errors.ts`, `server/utils/collections/shortcode.ts`, `server/utils/queries/collections.ts`, `server/utils/installs.test.ts` | Collections repository expands with CRUD operations (create/update/remove), item membership mutations, CollectionError typed error handling, shortcode generation for unique slugs, and read-only query helpers (listForOwner/listPublicPaged/listItems); mapCollectionError utility maps codes to HTTP status; test mock updated. |
| **Server API endpoints for collections operations** <br> `server/api/internal/collection-memberships.get.ts`, `server/api/internal/collections/[slug].get.ts`, `server/api/internal/collections/index.get.ts`, `server/api/internal/collections/index.post.ts`, `server/api/internal/collections/public.get.ts`, `server/api/internal/collections/[slug]/update.post.ts`, `server/api/internal/collections/[slug]/delete.post.ts`, `server/api/internal/collections/[slug]/items.post.ts`, `server/api/internal/collections/[slug]/items/[extensionId]/remove.post.ts`, `server/api/internal/profile/section.get.ts` | Twelve endpoints handle membership queries, collection CRUD, item add/remove, public pagination, and profile section integration with lazy system-collection seeding. |
| **Reusable UI components** <br> `app/components/collection/CollectionCard.vue`, `app/components/collection/CollectionForm.vue`, `app/components/extension/SaveToCollectionPopover.vue` | CollectionCard displays metadata with icon/visibility badge and owner; CollectionForm provides create/edit UI with i18n labels and visibility radio; SaveToCollectionPopover toggles membership with optimistic updates and inline collection creation. |
| **Page components and profile section integration** <br> `app/pages/collections/[slug].vue`, `app/pages/collections/index.vue`, `app/components/profile/SectionCollections.vue`, `app/components/profile/SectionRail.vue`, `app/pages/profile.vue` | Detail page handles view/edit/visibility/delete and item listing; index page browses public collections with pagination; SectionCollections renders user collections grid with create dialog; SectionRail extends profile nav; profile page wires section. |
| **Navigation integration and full localization** <br> `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue`, `app/components/extension/SaveButton.vue`, `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` | TopBar adds `/collections` link; SaveButton refactored to delegate to SaveToCollectionPopover; 50+ i18n strings for browsing, creation, detail view, actions, empty states, and ownership in English and Chinese. |

## Estimated code review effort

🎯 4 (Complex) | ⏱️ ~60 minutes

## Possibly related PRs

- [ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt#14](https://github.com/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pull/14): Establishes the foundational collections repository that this PR significantly expands with CRUD operations, error handling, and slug generation.
- [ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt#12](https://github.com/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pull/12): Introduces the profile section rail pattern that this PR extends by adding the `"collections"` section to the navigation.

## Poem

> A drawer for your tools both big and small,  
> Collections organize them all! 📚  
> Curate and share, public or just you—  
> Your extensions sorted through and through. ✨

_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_ | _⚡ Quick win_

<details>
<summary>🧩 Analysis chain</summary>

🌐 Web query:

`In Zod v4, when a field uses .default("private"), does objectSchema.partial().parse({}) still include that field by default?`

💡 Result:

Yes, in Zod v4, if a field uses.default("private"), calling objectSchema.partial.parse({}) will include that field in the resulting object with the default value assigned [1][2][3]. This behavior is an intentional design change in Zod v4 [1][2]. Unlike Zod 3, where.partial could effectively "clobber" or omit default values, Zod 4 ensures that defaults are always applied during parsing, even when the property is marked as optional [1][4][3]. This change was implemented to provide more consistent and predictable outcomes [1][2]. If you need to prevent the default value from appearing in the output when the field is missing (i.e., you want the field to remain omitted), a common workaround is to make the schema required first before applying.partial [1][2][3]: const schema = z.object({ status: z.string.default("private"), }); // Zod v4: Includes default value console.log(schema.partial.parse({})); // { status: "private" } // Workaround: Does not include default value console.log(schema.required.partial.parse({})); // {}

Citations:

- 1: https://github.com/colinhacks/zod/issues/5315
- 2: https://github.com/colinhacks/zod/issues/5079
- 3: https://github.com/colinhacks/zod/issues/5235
- 4: https://zod.dev/v4/changelog

</details>

**Fix `UpdateCollectionInput` so omitting `visibility` can’t reset collections to `private`**
In `shared/validators/collection.ts`, `CreateCollectionInput` sets `visibility` to `.default("private")`, and in Zod v4 `CreateCollectionInput.partial()` will still materialize that default when the field is omitted—so `UpdateCollectionInput.parse()` will often include `visibility: "private"`. `server/repositories/collections.ts` then patches `collections.visibility` whenever `input.visibility !== undefined`, which would overwrite a public collection back to private.
  
<details>
<summary>Proposed fix</summary>

```diff
 export const CreateCollectionInput = z.object({
@@
   visibility: CollectionVisibility.default("private"),
 })
@@
-export const UpdateCollectionInput = CreateCollectionInput.partial()
+export const UpdateCollectionInput = CreateCollectionInput
+  .omit({ visibility: true })
+  .partial()
+  .extend({
+    visibility: CollectionVisibility.optional(),
+  })
```
</details>

<details>
<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>

```
Verify each finding against current code. Fix only still-valid issues, skip the
rest with a brief reason, keep changes minimal, and validate.

In `@shared/validators/collection.ts` around lines 34 - 40, CreateCollectionInput
currently sets visibility with a default so calling
CreateCollectionInput.partial() for UpdateCollectionInput materializes that
default and can unintentionally reset a collection to "private"; change
UpdateCollectionInput to be derived without the defaulted visibility (e.g.,
start from the base shape or omit visibility from CreateCollectionInput before
making it partial) and then add visibility back as an optional field (so omitted
means "do not change")—update the validator symbol UpdateCollectionInput in
shared/validators/collection.ts and ensure server/repositories/collections.ts
still checks input.visibility !== undefined to decide whether to patch the DB.
```

</details>

> AGENT

One reviewer: **CodeRabbit AI**. 5 actionable findings + 6 nitpicks. Validate + Vercel preview both green. Bucketed:

**🟠 Real correctness issue (one):**
- **`UpdateCollectionInput` silently resets visibility to `private`** (`shared/validators/collection.ts:40`). In Zod **v4**, `CreateCollectionInput.partial()` *still* materializes the `.default("private")` for omitted fields. My update repo then patches whenever `input.visibility !== undefined` — so PATCHing `{ name: "foo" }` on a public collection would flip it private. This is a real bug. Fix: `.omit({ visibility: true }).partial().extend({ visibility: CollectionVisibility.optional() })`.

**🟠 Worth fixing (quick wins):**
- `index.post.ts` (create) doesn't wrap in `mapCollectionError` — sibling mutation endpoints do, asymmetric error shape.
- `AddItemInput` doesn't `.trim()` extensionId.
- `collection-memberships.get.ts` hand-validates instead of using Zod, unlike the rest.
- `public.get.ts` page param is unbounded — anon traffic could force deep offsets.
- `SaveToCollectionPopover` inline input has no `:maxlength`.

**🎨 CLAUDE.md design rule violations (decision #11 — accent reserved for primary CTAs):**
- TopBar `Collections` nav active state uses `bg-(--color-sidebar)/60` tinted fill.
- `SaveToCollectionPopover` "saved" state uses `--color-accent` border + tint.
- `/collections` pagination active chip uses `--color-accent`.

**⏭️ Defer / skip:**
- Race in `getOrCreateSystem` — known limitation, documented in the code comment, not introduced by this PR. Worth fixing later via a partial unique index.
- DB CHECK constraint for 10-char base36 slugs — would break the editorial seed (uses `sre-daily-kit`-style slugs). Skip.
- Localized-description guard in `[slug].vue` — already obsolete after the ExtCard refactor (in my unpushed commits).

Recommend applying the visibility-reset fix + the 5 quick wins + the 3 accent-color cleanups, all small. Want me to do that?
